### [1] [Sharp Neumann eigenvalue means on triangles:an exact certificate proof and stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38953)
*Guowei Dai,Yingxin Sun*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Laugesen–Siudeja 关于三角形前两个正 Neumann 特征值的猜想 6.34 中的两个不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证 Laugesen 与 Siudeja 提出的猜想 6.34，即关于三角形前两个正 Neumann 特征值的不等式及其极值刻画。

Method: 采用二维试验空间将问题归结为多项式正性判定；利用投影三次修正修复等周附近的二阶误差；其余形状由初等估计与有理 Bernstein 证书覆盖，并提供精确数据与验证程序。

Result: 证明了两条不等式：固定周长下等边三角形唯一最大化特征值的调和平均；固定面积下等边三角形唯一最大化其几何平均；并给出以边长非对称性表示的显式二次稳定性估计。

Conclusion: 猜想 6.34 成立，等边三角形在相应约束下为唯一极值, 方法可推广至相关特征值优化问题。

Abstract: We prove both inequalities in Laugesen and Siudeja's Conjecture~6.34 for the first two positive Neumann eigenvalues of triangles. The equilateral triangle uniquely maximizes their harmonic mean at fixed perimeter and their geometric mean at fixed area. Two-dimensional trial spaces reduce the proof to polynomial positivity. Projected cubic corrections repair the second-order error near equality; elementary estimates and rational Bernstein certificates cover the remaining shapes. We provide exact data and a verifier, and derive an explicit quadratic stability estimate in terms of side-length asymmetry.

</details>

### [2] [A Romanoff-type theorem for a multiset of products of powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38207)
*Artyom Radomskii*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究形如 $n=a+b_1^{u_1}\cdots b_d^{u_d}$ 的表示个数，在基 $b_i$ 无乘法独立或互素假设下，给出具有多表示的整数下界，并将结果应用于 $\mathcal{A}$ 为素数集与两平方和集的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前类似结果通常依赖基的乘法独立或互素假设，本文希望去掉这些限制，并统一处理底集 $\mathcal{A}$ 加 S-单位型移位表示的计数问题。

Method: 设固定整数 $b_1,\dots,b_d\ge2$，记其乘法秩为 $k$；对 $\mathcal{A}$ 施加密度与相关性假设，将不同指数元组分别计数，通过解析数论/筛法估计具有多表示的整数个数，得到以 $d,k$ 表示的下界。

Result: 在密度与相关性假设下，得到多表示整数个数的下界；特别地，当 $\mathcal{A}$ 为素数集时，正比例 $n\le x$ 至少有 $c_1(\log x)^{d-1}$ 个表示；当 $\mathcal{A}$ 为两平方和可表示正整数集时，至少有 $c_1(\log x)^{d-1/2}$ 个表示。

Conclusion: 无需基的乘法独立或互素假设，即可建立统一的多表示计数下界，并给出素数集及两平方和集的显式应用。

Abstract: Let $b_1,\dots,b_d\ge2$ be fixed integers, and let $k$ be their multiplicative rank. We study representations $n=a+b_1^{u_1}\cdots b_d^{u_d}$, where $a$ belongs to a set $\mathcal{A}$ of positive integers and $u_1,\dots,u_d$ are positive integers, counting distinct tuples of exponents separately. Under density and correlation assumptions on $\mathcal{A}$, we obtain a lower bound for the number of integers with many such representations, in terms of $d$ and $k$. In particular, when $\mathcal{A}$ is the set of primes or the set of positive integers representable as a sum of two squares, a positive proportion of the integers $n\le x$ have at least $c_1(\log x)^{d-1}$ or $c_1(\log x)^{d-1/2}$ representations, respectively, for some $c_1>0$ and all sufficiently large $x$. No multiplicative independence or coprimality assumptions on the bases are required.

</details>


### [3] [Strong Artin Conjecture for Generalized Octahedral Representations in $\mathrm{GL}_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38231)
*Junwu Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了投影像为 $C_3^2\rtimes \mathrm{SL}(2,3)$ 的三维不可约可解 Artin 表示满足强 Artin 猜想，即三维中最后一个可解情形，并据此推出全部六维本原可解 Artin 表示的强 Artin 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维可解 Artin 表示中，投影像为 $C_3^2\rtimes \mathrm{SL}(2,3)$ 的情形是唯一遗留、也是最大的可解情形。解决它即可完成三维可解情形的完整分类性验证，并作为进一步推广的基石。

Method: 针对无中间域的非 Galois 四次扩张，建立新的基变换（base change）与自守诱导（automorphic induction）情形，用以构造相应 Artin 表示的对应自守表示，从而验证其 $L$-函数的解析性质。

Result: 证明了该最大可解三维 Artin 表示的强 Artin 猜想，从而闭合三维所有可解情形；并由此推出所有六维本原可解 Artin 表示的强 Artin 猜想。

Conclusion: 三维可解 Artin 情形被彻底解决（含最后一个情形），提升了强 Artin 猜想在低维可解表示中的验证范围。

Abstract: We prove the strong Artin conjecture for the largest solvable Artin representations in three dimensions, whose projective image is isomorphic to the affine special linear group $C_3^2\rtimes \mathrm{SL}(2, 3)$. It is also the last solvable case in three dimensions. This is achieved with a new case of base change and automorphic induction for non-Galois quartic extensions with no intermediate fields. We additionally deduce the strong Artin conjecture for all 6-dimensional primitive solvable Artin representations.

</details>


### [4] [Large gaps between Romanoff numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38408)
*Artyom Radomskii*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究不含Romanoff数（形如p+2^n，p为素数）的最长区间的长度下界。作者利用截断的除数和的乘积，证明了$G_{\mathcal{R}}(X)\gg\log\log X$，改进了Kalmynin和Konyagin此前$G_{\mathcal{R}}(X)\gg\log\log X/\log\log\log X$的结果。关键步骤是将平移引理推广到$O(\log X)$个整平移的集合上，并适用于任意固定整数$a\ge2$的$p+a^n$情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Romanoff证明了可表示为素数加2的幂的整数集合具有正下密度，而Kalmynin和Konyagin从互补方向研究了不含此类表示的长区间。作者希望改进其关于最长无Romanoff数区块下界的估计。

Method: 使用截断的除数和的乘积（truncated products of divisor sums）。关键步骤是将平移引理（translate lemma）推广到$O(\log X)$个绝对值不超过$X$的整平移的集合上：在可忽略代价下移除整除平移差的辅助素数，经初步筛法后可同时覆盖所有剩余平移。

Result: 证明了$G_{\mathcal{R}}(X)\gg\log\log X$，改进了Kalmynin和Konyagin的$G_{\mathcal{R}}(X)\gg\log\log X/\log\log\log X$。该论证是无条件的，且同样适用于每个固定整数$a\ge2$的$p+a^n$情形。

Conclusion: 作者成功将无Romanoff数的最长区块下界从$\log\log X/\log\log\log X$提升至$\log\log X$，方法具有无条件性，并自然推广到$p+a^n$（固定$a\ge2$）的更一般情形。

Abstract: Questions concerning representations of integers as the sum of a prime and a power of two go back to the correspondence between Euler and Goldbach in 1752 and to de Polignac's work of 1849. Romanoff proved that the set of integers admitting such a representation has positive lower density. Following the complementary direction studied by Kalmynin and Konyagin, we consider long intervals containing no Romanoff numbers. We use truncated products of divisor sums to derive the bound $G_{\mathcal{R}}(X)\gg\log\log X$ for the longest block in $[1,X]$ containing no integer of the form $p+2^n$, with $p$ prime and $n\ge1$. This improves the bound $G_{\mathcal{R}}(X)\gg\log\log X/\log\log\log X$ of Kalmynin and Konyagin. The key step extends a translate lemma to sets of $O(\log X)$ integer shifts of absolute value at most $X$: auxiliary primes dividing differences of shifts are removed at negligible cost. After preliminary sieving, this allows all remaining shifts to be covered simultaneously. The argument is unconditional and also applies to $p+a^n$ for every fixed integer $a\ge2$.

</details>


### [5] [Euclidean Triangles Associated with Diophantine $D(4)$-triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38544)
*Marija Bliznac Trebješanin,Jelena Pleština*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究 Diophantine D(4)-三元组对应的欧几里得三角形，证明了长度 r=√(ab+4)、s=√(ac+4)、t=√(bc+4) 构成非退化三角形的充要条件，并对固定最短边 r 给出了三角形分类、计数判据与角型判定，还证明了相似必全等及 Heronian 三角形的 Pell 方程刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D(4)-三元组是数论中 Diophantine 三元组的经典对象，而由 √(ab+4) 等表达式定义的长度自然联系到几何中的三角形。作者希望建立 D(4)-三元组与欧几里得三角形之间的对应关系，回答这些长度何时构成三角形、有多少个、形状如何，以及其中特殊三角形（如 Heronian 三角形）如何用经典丢番图方程描述。

Method: 对 D(4)-三元组 {a,b,c}（a<b<c），考察长度 r=√(ab+4)、s=√(ac+4)、t=√(bc+4)；通过代数与数论分析（含三元组正则性、r^2-4 的因子分解、约数条件）判定非退化三角形条件并计数；利用相似性与全等性论证；对族 {r-2, r+2, 4r} 借助 Pell 方程刻画 Heronian 三角形。

Result: 1) r、s、t 构成非退化三角形当且仅当该三元组是正则的且 r>b-a。2) 对固定最短边 r，用 r^2-4 的因子分解描述所有对应三角形，给出其数目的约数判据，并确定其角型。3) 两个相似的相关三角形必全等。4) 对族 {r-2, r+2, 4r}，得到相关 Heronian 三角形的 Pell 方程刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 D(4)-三元组与三角形之间的系统联系，给出了存在性、计数、角型及刚性（相似即全等）的完整刻画，并通过 Pell 方程揭示了 Heronian 三角形与 D(4)-三元组族的深层结构关系。

Abstract: In this paper we study Euclidean triangles associated with Diophantine $D(4)$-triples. For a $D(4)$-triple $\{a,b,c\}$, $a<b<c$, we prove that the lengths $r=\sqrt{ab+4}$, $s=\sqrt{ac+4}$ and $t=\sqrt{bc+4}$ associated with this $D(4)$-triple form a non-degenerate triangle if and only if the triple is regular and $r>b-a$. For a fixed shortest side $r$, we describe all triangles associated with $D(4)$-triples in terms of the factorisations of $r^2-4$, give a divisor criterion for their number, and determine their angle type. We also prove that two similar triangles associated with $D(4)$-triples are necessarily congruent. Finally, for the family of $D(4)$-triples $\{r-2,r+2,4r\}$, we obtain a Pell-equation characterisation of the Heronian triangles associated with these $D(4)$-triples.

</details>


### [6] [Zeta elements for $p$-ordinary modular forms over imaginary quadratic fields and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38558)
*Ruichen Xu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在权重大于等于2的p-ordinary尖点新形式上证明了Kato的Iwasawa主猜想，仅需big-image假设与剩余表示绝对不可约性，推广了Burungale–Castella–Skinner关于椭圆曲线的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burungale–Castella–Skinner已对Q上具有好普通约化的椭圆曲线证明Kato主猜想，本文希望将该结果推广到更高权重的模形式，填补权重大于2情形的空白。

Method: 沿用Burungale–Castella–Skinner的总体策略；关键新要素是在p分裂的虚二次域上构造附属于f的zeta元素，并证明其显式互反律，推广Burungale–Skinner–Tian–Wan的工作。

Result: 在big-image假设与剩余表示绝对不可约的假设下，证明了偶权重k=2r≥2、水平N、p∤N且平凡nebentype的p-ordinary尖点新形式的Kato Iwasawa主猜想，该猜想以f的p进L函数表述。

Conclusion: 本文为Burungale–Castella–Skinner论证中的关键输入提供了更高权重的类比，使该方法可推广至更高权重模形式。

Abstract: Let $p\geq 5$ be a prime, and let $f$ be a $p$-ordinary cuspidal newform of even weight $k = 2r \geq 2$ and level $N$, with $p \nmid N$ and trivial nebentype. In this article, we prove Kato's Iwasawa main conjecture for $f$, formulated in terms of the $p$-adic $L$-function of $f$, under the sole assumptions that the associated Galois representation of $f$ satisfies the big-image hypothesis and that its residual representation is absolutely irreducible. This generalises the main result of Burungale--Castella--Skinner, which treats elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Q}$ with good ordinary reduction at $p$. Our proof follows their general strategy. A key new ingredient is the construction, over an imaginary quadratic field in which $p$ splits, of a zeta element attached to $f$, together with a proof of its explicit reciprocity laws, generalising the corresponding work of Burungale--Skinner--Tian--Wan. These results provide the higher-weight analogue of a crucial input in the argument of Burungale--Castella--Skinner and allow their method to be carried out for higher-weight modular forms.

</details>


### [7] [Monogenity of the irreducible factors of $(k,t)$-Fibonacci and $(k,t)$-Lucas polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38844)
*Michail Karatarakis,Sumandeep Kaur*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究广义(k,t)-Fibonacci与(k,t)-Lucas多项式序列的不可约因子的根所生成数域的单生成性，借助Dickson多项式及其与分圆域的联系证明主要结果，并特化为经典Fibonacci、Lucas多项式及第一、二类Chebyshev多项式的单生成性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数域的单生成性是代数数论中的核心性质之一，决定整数环是否存在幂基。经典Fibonacci与Lucas多项式对应的单生成性已有结果，但广义(k,t)参数族的单生成性尚缺乏系统刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白并统一已有结论。

Method: 以广义(k,t)-Fibonacci和(k,t)-Lucas多项式的不可约因子的根生成的数域为对象，利用Dickson多项式及其与分圆域之间的显式联系来判定数域的单生成性。

Result: 建立了广义(k,t)-Fibonacci与(k,t)-Lucas多项式序列相应数域的单生成性判据；作为特例，重新得到经典Fibonacci与Lucas多项式的单生成性结果，并给出第一类和第二类Chebyshev多项式对应的单生成性结果。

Conclusion: Dickson多项式与分圆域的联系为处理该类多项式生成的数域的单生成性提供了有效工具，所得结果统一并推广了经典情形，同时覆盖Chebyshev多项式情形。

Abstract: In this article, we study the monogenity of the number field generated by a root of an irreducible factor of the generalized $(k,t)$-Fibonacci and $(k,t)$-Lucas polynomial sequence. We use Dickson polynomials and their connection with cyclotomic fields to prove our main results. As special cases, we recover the monogenity results for the classical Fibonacci and Lucas polynomials and obtain corresponding monogenity results for Chebyshev polynomials of the first and second kinds.

</details>


### [8] [On a problem of Nathanson related to strongly minimal asymptotic bases of order $h$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38865)
*Shi-Qiang Chen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了存在阶为 h 的强极小渐近基 A，其计数函数满足 A(x) ≍ x^θ（1/h<θ≤1/(h-1)），从而解决了 Nathanson 于1988年提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nathanson 于1988年提出关于强极小渐近基的存在性及其计数函数渐近阶的问题，此前缺乏对给定阶数 h 下达到指定增长阶 θ 的构造，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过加性数论中的渐近基与极小基构造技术，对任意整数 h≥2 及参数 θ∈(1/h,1/(h-1)]，显式构造一个阶为 h 的强极小渐近基 A，并证明其计数函数 A(x) 与 x^θ 同阶。

Result: 证明了存在阶为 h 的强极小渐近基 A，使得 A(x) ≍ x^θ，即其计数函数满足双侧渐近估计，达到该参数区间内的最优增长阶。

Conclusion: 该结果完整回答了 Nathanson 1988年提出的问题，表明在 θ∈(1/h,1/(h-1)] 范围内强极小渐近基可以达到任意指定增长阶，完善了渐近基极小性理论。

Abstract: Let $h\geq2$ be an integer and let $1/h<θ\leq1/(h-1)$. In this paper, we prove that there exists a strongly minimal asymptotic basis $A$ of order $h$ such that $A(x)\asymp x^θ$. This solves a problem posed by Nathanson in 1988.

</details>


### [9] [The twisted convolution identity and ghost $r$-SICs from finite quantum dilogarithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39192)
*Marcus Appleby,Steven T. Flammia,Gene S. Kopp*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 将RW关于秩1情形的有限五边形关系证明推广到所有秩r可容许元组，建立模量子二重对数与Shintani-Faddeev模余圈约定的对应字典，从而在特定数论条件下构造出ghost r-SICs，并在Stark猜想下与Hermitian r-SICs（r-SIC-POVMs）伽罗瓦共轭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RW最近对模量子二重对数的实二次特殊值证明了有限五边形关系，并据此建立了秩1扭曲卷积恒等式，但其显式论证仅在主情形给出，虽声称对所有秩1可容许元组成立，却未展开。本文旨在突破秩1限制，将结果推广到任意秩r，并为跨文献的两种约定提供统一字典，进而构造更高秩的SIC型构型。

Method: 将RW在秩1主情形下的显式论证推广至所有秩r可容许元组；建立模量子二重对数与Shintani-Faddeev模余圈两种约定之间的显式对应字典；利用由此得到的秩r扭曲卷积恒等式，在满足r<(d-1)/2且d²-1/[r(d-r)]∈ℤ的正整数d,r条件下构造ghost r-SICs。

Result: 证明RW的五边形关系论证对所有秩r可容许元组成立，并给出两种约定的显式字典；证明当d,r为正整数且满足r<(d-1)/2、d²-1/[r(d-r)]∈ℤ时，存在由ℂ^d中d²个秩r子空间构成的ghost r-SICs，满足非Hermitian等弦条件；在Stark猜想下，这些构型与称为r-SICs（即秩r SIC-POVMs）的Hermitian等弦构型互为伽罗瓦共轭。

Conclusion: 该工作把RW的秩1结果系统推广到任意秩r，统一了模量子二重对数与Shintani-Faddeev模余圈两种约定，并把五边形关系转化为构造非Hermitian等弦构型（ghost r-SICs）的工具；在Stark猜想下，该构造与Hermitian r-SIC-POVMs通过伽罗瓦共轭相联系，揭示了SIC-POVM存在性与数论（Stark猜想）之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Radchenko and Wheeler (RW) recently proved a finite pentagon relation for real quadratic special values of the modular quantum dilogarithm and used it to establish the rank-$1$ twisted convolution identity conjectured by the current authors. RW gave an explicit argument in the principal case and remarked that their proof holds for all rank-$1$ admissible tuples. We extend their proof to all rank-$r$ admissible tuples and provide an explicit dictionary between the modular quantum dilogarithm and the Shintani-Faddeev modular cocycle conventions in the respective papers. Thus, we establish that, if $d,r$ are positive integers such that $r<\frac{d-1}{2}$ and $\frac{d^2-1}{r(d-r)} \in \mathbb{Z}$, then there exist ghost $r$-SICs: i.e., configurations of $d^2$ rank-$r$ subspaces in $\mathbb{C}^d$ that satisfy a non-Hermitian equichordal condition. Under the Stark conjecture, these configurations are Galois conjugate to Hermitian equichordal configurations called $r$-SICs (or rank-$r$ SIC-POVMs).

</details>


### [10] [The cardinality of a set containing the pairwise sums of four positive integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39314)
*Wouter van Doorn,John Erlbacher*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了对于 $A\subseteq\{1,\ldots,2n\}$ 且 $|A|\ge n+4$（$n\ge6$），必存在四个不同正整数其两两和均在 $A$ 中，常数 $C=4$ 最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study random polynomials of the form $R(x)=x^n+ω_{n-1}x^{n-1}+\cdots+ω_0$, where $ω_0,\dots,ω_{n-1}$ are independent, uniformly bounded integer-valued random variables, and $ω_1,\dots,ω_{n-1}$ have a fixed common law $μ$.
  We prove (unconditionally) that, if the Rényi entropy of order $2$ satisfies $H_2(μ)=-\log\|μ\|_2^2>12$, then $\mathbb{P}(\operatorname{disc}(R)\text{ is a square})=O_μ(1/\log n)$. Combined with previous results, this shows that, for such measures $μ$ and under additional hypotheses, $R$ has full Galois group $\mathrm{Sym}(n)$ with high probability, when conditioned on $ω_0\ne 0$.

Abstract: Quadratic Chabauty computations for $X_0(N)^*$ are complicated by local height contributions at primes of bad reduction. As our first main result, we show that such a $p$-adic height exists for all squarefree levels $N>714$. Each component of this model gives rise to a linear condition which ensures that the contribution at that component vanishes. Using embeddings of quadratic orders into quaternion algebras, we obtain an upper bound for the number of these conditions; when the genus of $X_0(N)^*$ is greater than one more than this bound, there is enough freedom to choose a suitable correspondence, simplifying quadratic Chabauty computations. As an application, we determine the rational points on several curves $X_0(N)^*$ for which this was not done before.

</details>

### [25] [H-principle for corank two distributions of odd rank and maximal first Kronecker index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38214)
*Milan Jovanovic,Igor Zelenko*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究奇数维余秩二分布（余秩二中相当于接触/偶接触分布），证明其微分关系是ample的，从而满足多参数C^0-close h-原理，并给出(2N+3)维流形存在此类分布的完全拓扑刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在余秩二情形下，寻找接触分布与偶接触分布之外的、由开Diff-不变关系定义的新分布类，并研究其h-原理与存在性拓扑障碍。已有成果中仅偶接触分布（McDuff 1987）和最大小增长向量分布（Martínez-Aguinaga 2026）被验证ample，本文填补该空白。

Method: 考虑奇数秩2N+1的余秩二分布D，其相伴斜对称形式束落在自然GL(D(q))作用的通有轨道内（等价于D具有最大第一Kronecker指数）。通过对束在超平面上的限制使用Kronecker-Weierstrass标准形，将ample性验证归结为计算实二元形式对空间中结式超曲面补集的连通分支的凸包，分支由绕数（经典Cauchy指数）分离。

Result: 证明所研究的微分关系是ample的，因而满足多参数C^0-close h-原理；由此得到(2N+3)维流形容许此类分布的完全拓扑刻画，且该判据对不可平行化流形同样成立。对N≥2，本文的类是最大小增长向量分布类的真开子类，由束上的额外条件区分。

Conclusion: 余秩二最大第一Kronecker指数分布构成一个新的ample分布类，其ample性验证需要计算结式超曲面补集各连通分支的凸包，这与McDuff偶接触情形中待移除集为thin集、补集自动连通而ample性立即成立形成对比。

Abstract: We study corank-two distributions $D$ of odd rank $2N+1$ whose associated pencil of skew-symmetric forms $\{dα|_{D}(q)\mid α\inΩ^1(M),\ α|_{D}=0\}$ lies, at every point, in the generic orbit of the natural $\mathrm{GL}\bigl(D(q)\bigr)$-action; equivalently, $D$ has maximal first Kronecker index. In corank two, this class is the analogue of contact and even-contact distributions. We prove that the corresponding differential relation is ample and hence satisfies the multi-parametric $C^0$-close $h$-principle, and we deduce a complete topological characterization for a $(2N+3)$-dimensional manifold to admit such a distribution. The criterion is satisfied by non-parallelizable manifolds as well. To the best of our knowledge, ampleness has been verified so far for two classes of distributions defined by an open $\mathrm{Diff}$-invariant relation: even-contact distributions (McDuff, 1987) and distributions of rank greater than two with maximal small growth vector, in arbitrary ambient dimension (Martínez-Aguinaga, 2026); for $N\ge2$, our class is a proper open subclass of the latter, singled out by a further condition on the pencil. The verification of ampleness reduces, via Kronecker--Weierstrass normal forms of the restrictions of the pencil to hyperplanes, to computing the convex hulls of the connected components of the complement of the resultant hypersurface in the space of pairs of real binary forms of fixed degrees, components separated by a winding number, i.e., by the classical Cauchy index. This is in contrast with the even-contact case of McDuff, where the set to be removed is thin (i.e., of codimension at least two), so that its complement is automatically connected and ampleness is immediate.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 渐屈线保持正则，并在各向同性点（$q=0$）与曲线相切，而在复拐点（$\kappa=0$）趋于无穷；$A_2$ 尖点被空间参数普适展开；$A_3$ 燕尾奇点被 $(x_1,x_2,\theta)$ 全纯普适展开当且仅当复非退化条件 $\kappa^4+\kappa_s^2\neq0$ 成立；简单各向同性点导致该普适性失效，产生复燕尾曲面的退化非横截截面。

Conclusion: 复非退化条件 $\kappa^4+\kappa_s^2\neq0$ 是 $A_3$ 燕尾奇点全纯普适展开的充要条件；简单各向同性点是普适性破裂的根源。

Abstract: We study the local geometry, singularity classification, and wavefront dynamics of complex evolutoids for regular holomorphic curves in $\mathbb{C}^2$ equipped with the standard complex bilinear metric. By integrating the generating family of rotated complex affine lines, we derive an explicit parametrization for the 1-parameter family of complex wavefronts $Γ^{θ, w}$ and establish that their singular cusp locus sweeps out the complex evolutoid $E^θ$. Using a square-root-free formulation, we classify the local behavior of $E^θ$, proving that evolutoids remain regular and tangent to the curve at isotropic points ($q=0$) while escaping to infinity at complex inflections ($κ=0$). Finally, we analyze the three-dimensional complex discriminant surface $\mathcal{D}_F \subset \mathbb{C}^3$ formed by the evolving family $E^θ$. We prove that $A_2$ cusps are versally unfolded by spatial parameters, whereas $A_3$ swallowtail singularities are holomorphically versally unfolded by $(x_1, x_2, θ)$ if and only if the complex non-degeneracy condition $κ^4 + κ_s^2 \neq 0$ holds. Crucially, we demonstrate that simple isotropic points drive the breakdown of this versality condition, inducing degenerate, non-transverse sections of the complex swallowtail surface.

</details>

Abstract: We study a two-parameter deformation of scalar curvature associated with the Gauduchon connections on compact Hermitian manifolds. A central identity expresses its dependence on the connection parameter through the squared norm of the trace-free part of the torsion of the Chern connection. We establish existence criteria for metrics of constant deformed scalar curvature and analyze the global dependence of the deformed Yamabe invariant on the connection parameter, including when the critical threshold is reached. Applications include attainment of the critical threshold for an explicit Hopf example in every complex dimension at least two and on non-locally conformally Kähler Iwasawa products.

Abstract: The Gaussian curvature of the graphs of cubic polynomials $f(z)=(z-a)(z-b)(z-c)$, with triangle of zeros $\triangle=\triangle(a,b,c)$, is analyzed. We introduce the composed Pythagorean mean $\mathtt{B}_{\triangle}$, a combination of the three classical Pythagorean means, which plays a central role in the description of the critical points of the Gaussian curvature. When $\triangle$ is not equilateral, the curvature has exactly five critical points: a global maximum at the barycenter, two local minima, and two saddle points, forming a rhombus whose vertices lie on the axes of the Siebeck-Marden ellipse of the triangle. We also initiate a comparative study of $\mathtt{B}_{\triangle}$ with the skew of the triangle by describing a family of polynomials whose zeros determine a specific family of isosceles triangles. Abstract: Following the recent work of M. Womack \cite{Wo26} we consider the steady Ricci Yang-Mills solitons on 2d surfaces containing orbifold point $\mathbb{R}^2/\mathbb{Z}_p$. We show that there are a family of solitons depending parameter $λ$ which approaches to $\mathbb{Z}_p$-quotient of Hamilton cigar soliton as $λ\to (-2/p)^+$ and approaches to (after rescaling) $\mathbb{Z}_p$-quotient of round sphere as $λ\to \infty$. For any integer $q >p$ there is a $λ$ whose corresponding soliton metric is defined on football orbifold $S_{p,q}^2$.

Abstract: Inspired by the Green-function monotonicity formulas of Colding, Minicozzi, and Manea, we establish weighted monotonicity formulas on closed weighted Riemannian manifolds satisfying ${\rm Ric}_f^m \ge (m-1)kg$, where $m>n\ge 3$ and $k>0$. We also treat the case of negative effective dimension $m<0$, for which the nonnegative square decomposition remains valid, while the change in the behavior near the Green pole leads to reversed area and volume monotonicity on finite sublevel sets. Method: 以映射F的微分dF的非零奇异值λ_1,...,λ_r构造奇异值函数S(λ)=∑λ_i²/(1+λ_i²)，通过分析Θ(F)的comass与S之间的关系进行证明，并结合秩与仿射性讨论二分情形。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了Θ(F)的comass条件，揭示了S≤1的二分结构，并将超曲面的校准平面唯一性推广至一般映射，同时给出了S≡1时的映射刚性结论。

Abstract: For a map $F:Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^n\to\mathbb{R}^m$, let $Θ(F)$ be the $n$-form on $Ω\times\mathbb{R}^m$ introduced by Tsai and Wang [arXiv:2604.04336]. If $λ_1,\ldots,λ_{r}$ are the nonzero singular values of $d F$ at any fixed point, we prove that $$
  Θ(F) \text{ has comass one if and only if }~
  \mathcal{S}(λ):=\sum_{i=1}^{r}\frac{λ_i^2}{1+λ_i^2}\leq1. $$
  Note that if $\text{rank} d F\le1$, $\mathcal{S}$ is always less than $1$. We also prove a dichotomy that if $\mathcal{S}\leq 1$, then either $\mathcal{S} < 1$ or $\mathcal{S}\equiv1$. Abstract: An old conjecture in non-Kähler geometry states that if the Chern holomorphic sectional curvature of a compact Hermitian manifold is equal to a constant $c$, then the metric must be Kähler when $c\neq 0$ and be Chern flat when $c=0$. The conjecture is known to be true in dimension two by the work of Balas--Gauduchon and Apostolov--Davidov--Muškarov in the 1980s and 1990s. Recently, Qin and Tian proved the conjecture in complex dimension three when $c\neq 0$, and the $c=0$ case was proved by Chen--Li under the additional assumption that the metric is balanced. Abstract: Naff's planarity estimate is a crucial tool for analysing singularities of quadratically pinched mean curvature flows in high codimension, showing that high-curvature regions become asymptotically codimension one. More precisely, we show that the isotropy algebra decomposes into eigenspaces corresponding to nonnegative eigenvalues of an adjoint endomorphism, with zero eigenspace given by its reductive part. This extends the classical decomposition for the complexified action of $U^{\mathbb C}$ to the real reductive setting. As an application, we prove that the identity component of the compact stabilizer at critical points of $f\circμ_{\mathfrak p}$ is a maximal compact subgroup of the identity component of the $G$-stabilizer.

Abstract: We investigate Lie groups endowed with left-invariant Lorentzian metrics of nonzero constant sectional curvature. TL;DR: 本文证明 Baricz 关于第一类 Bessel 函数的猜想，推广 Szász 的经典 Turán 型不等式，并将结果扩展到一般 Bessel 函数、正则 Coulomb 波函数、虚阶修正 Bessel 函数与抛物柱函数，同时给出复数域上的包含性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进并推广第一类 Bessel 函数的经典 Turán 型不等式，证明 Á. Baricz 的猜想，并统一处理多类振荡特殊函数的 Turán 型不等式问题。

Method: 考虑第一类 Bessel 函数的归一化 Turán 表达式，证明其在相邻零点之间分支存在局部极小值，且极小值序列严格递增收敛、关于阶严格递减；将方法推广到一般 Bessel 函数、正则 Coulomb 波函数、虚阶修正 Bessel 函数和抛物柱函数；并用 Wilf 意义下的从属因子序列技术证明复数包含性质。

Conclusion: 所提方法具有通用性，可用于处理其他振荡特殊函数的 Turánian 或归一化 Turánian 相关问题。

Abstract: In this paper our aim is to prove a conjecture of Á. Baricz on Bessel functions of the first kind, which improves the classical Turán type inequality for Bessel functions of the first kind proved by O. Szász. The idea is to consider the normalized Turán expression for Bessel functions of the first kind and to show that between two consecutive zeros of the Bessel functions of the first kind the branch of this normalized Turán expression has a local minimum, and the minimum values form a strictly increasing convergent sequence, whose terms are strictly decreasing with respect to the order. Moreover, we extend this result to general Bessel functions and regular Coulomb wave functions, and we use a similar approach to show sharp Turán type inequalities for modified Bessel functions of purely imaginary order and parabolic cylinder functions. In addition, we prove a complex analogue of the result on Bessel functions of the first kind about the strictly decreasing property of the successive minimum values: an inclusion property in the complex plane of the Turán expression for Bessel functions of the first kind by using the subordinating factor sequence technique in the sense of H.S. Wilf. The techniques employed in the paper may be useful to treat similar problems where Turánians or normalized Turánians of other oscillatory special functions appear.

</details>

Abstract: We prove sharp volume bounds (up to dimensional constants) for the $δ$-neighbourhoods of a general family of Besicovitch-Rado-Kinney (BRK) type sets associated with surfaces of revolution in $\mathbb R^n$, $n\ge 3$, which, in particular, include spheres, elliptic paraboloids, and cones. In $\mathbb R^3$, it is worth noting that our result necessitates a variant of Sogge's cinematic curvature condition.

Abstract: In 1943, R. Salem asked whether the Fourier-Stieltjes transform of the Minkowski question-mark function vanishes at infinity. This problem was answered affirmatively by Jordan and Sahlsten in 2016 as a consequence of their general results on Gibbs measures for the Gauss map. In this note we give a self-contained proof for ?(x). Several aspects of our argument are substantially different, making a very short proof possible in the ?(x) case. Moreover, we obtain an explicit polynomial decay estimate, with exponent at least 0.0472. We then prove the existence of the correlation dimension of the Minkowski measure, thereby improving the previous upper bound for the optimal Fourier decay exponent from 0.4373 to 0.4223.

Abstract: Nikodym constructed a measure-zero Borel set in the plane that contains a punctured line segment passing through each point in the unit square. Fenton and Falconer asked whether a similar construction is possible when ``lines through a point'' are replaced by ``circles or circular arcs with a prescribed center.'' This was answered in the negative by Bourgain and Marstrand. We study generalizations of this question, in which ``lines through a point'' or ``circles with prescribed center'' are replaced by more general analytic families of plane curves.
  As a key ingredient, we prove new maximal function and local smoothing estimates for families of curves satisfying a multi-parameter analogue of Sogge's cinematic curvature condition; this allows us to establish the non-existence of Nikodym-type sets in certain specific settings. The result also resolves a local smoothing conjecture by the sixth author. In the other direction, we use a recent nonlinear generalization of Falconer's prescribed projection theorem to construct Nikodym-type sets in other specific settings. Combining these ingredients with tools from tame geometry, we precisely characterize when Nikodym-type sets exist for analytic families of plane curves.

</details>

Abstract: Let $p\in[1,\infty)$ and $δ\in(0,n]$.
  Recently, to characterize the weighted boundedness of maximal operator on Choquet integrals based on Hausdorff content $\mathcal H_\infty^δ$, a class of Capacity Muckenhoupt weights is introduced, denoted by $\mathcal A_{p,δ}$, and,
  for any fixed $δ\in(0,n]$,
  this new class of weights is proved to satisfy the self-improving property with respect to the index $p\in(1,\infty)$.
  In this note, we show that, for every fixed $p\in[1,\infty)$,
  the class of capacity Muckenhoupt weights $\mathcal A_{p,δ}$ fails to satisfy the self-improving property with respect to the index $δ\in(0,n]$.

</details>

Abstract: The optimal Sobolev regularity of the roots of a smooth curve of monic complex polynomials is $W^{1,q}$, for $q \in [1,\frac{d}{d-1})$, where $d$ is the degree. This result is stable in the sense that the solution map from $C^d$ coefficients to $W^{1,q}$ roots is continuous. We prove that, after a natural Lipschitz reparameterization, the roots are Lipschitz and the map from $C^d$ coefficients to reparameterization and reparameterized roots is continuous with respect to the $W^{1,q}$ topology on the target spaces, for all $q \in [1,\infty)$. The result is based on a convergence and reparameterization theorem for Almgren's $Q$-valued Sobolev functions.

</details>

Abstract: We show that every irreducible linear differential equations on the Riemann sphere with regular and/or unramified irregular singularities can be transformed into either the trivial equation or a Fuchsian system of $E_8$-fundamental spectral type by a sequence of invertible transformations consisting of confluences, unfoldings, Laplace transformations, gauge transformations and Möbius transformations. The $E_8$-spectral type is uniquely determined by the original equation. A Fuchsian system of $E_8$-fundamental spectral type has three singular points, 0, 1, and $\infty$. The degrees of the minimal polynomials of the residue matrices at 0 and 1 are two and three, respectively, and the sum of the maximal dimensions of eigenspaces of the three residue matrices is not greater than the size of the matrices. The result follows from the correspondence between spectral types of Fuchsian systems and roots of a star-shaped Kac-Moody root system.

Abstract: We define a family of random sets of points on the sphere $\mathbb S^2$, the Pearl ensemble, depending on several parameters. The expected value of the logarithmic energy can be computed exactly up to terms of order $O(\sqrt{N}\log N)$, with $N$ the number of points. For properly chosen values of the parameters, the coefficient of the linear term in the expansion of the logarithmic energy can be taken as close as desired to the value $(1-\log 3)/2\sim -0.0493061\ldots$. Abstract: In a bulk ferromagnet, magnetic energy is six orders of magnitude weaker than
  elastic energy. Slenderness removes this disparity. In a thin ferromagnetic rod, the two energies
  scale differently with aspect ratio, and an ordinary nickel wire deforms nonlinearly even
  under modest remote fields. We develop a three-dimensional variational theory of a
  ferromagnetic Kirchhoff rod under gravity and magnetic loading and study it through
  bifurcation analysis, computation, and experiment. A uniform field can stabilize a vertical rod
  against buckling under its own weight, the classical Greenhill instability. We give sharp
  criteria for clamped and pinned supports in terms of an Airy-type principal
  eigenvalue, and give a fine description of the character of the bifurcation landscape, which has qualitatively different features than its non-magnetic counterpart. When subject to the field generated by a
  permanent magnet, we identify a rich bifurcation landscape as the magnet is rotated quasistatically along a circle of radius slightly larger than the rest length of the rod. We prove that instability occurs only through a
  planar mode and that each snap is a nondegenerate saddle-node. We also locate the two
  folds that produce hysteresis.

  Greenhill threshold, restabilization at ~$30$ mT for both clamped and pinned supports, as well as the salient features of the hysteresis loop such as switching angles, width, and geometry of folds accurately.

Method: 借鉴作者先前关于IPM方程工作\cite{BCMZ2025}的思路，选取在原点附近消失的初始密度扰动以中和稳定分层；通过迭代逼近格式构造在原点处具有强变形的近似序列。核心区别在于此处演化由密度与涡度的非线性耦合驱动。

Abstract: We establish instantaneous $H^2$ norm explosion for the two-dimensional Boussinesq equations around the linearly stable background profile $-x_2$. While previous ill-posedness results for this system relied on $L^\infty$-type spaces, this work provides the first strong ill-posedness result in the energy-critical space, where stable background stratification acts to suppress deformation growth.

Crucially, unlike the IPM setting, the evolution is here driven by the non-linear coupling between density and vorticity. Since the initial vorticity vanishes identically, the instantaneous loss of regulari\-ty is driven by density-vorticity feedback - a mechanism fundamentally distinct from both $H^1$ Euler ill-posedness of Bourgain \& Li and the IPM evolution.
  Through an iterative scheme yielding a sequence of approximations with strong deformation at the origin, we construct a strong solution $(ρ(t), ω(t))$ originating from data with arbitrarily small $H^2 \times (H^1\cap \dot H^{-1})$ norm, which immediately exhibits $H^2 \times H^1$ norm explosion for all $t > 0$ while maintaining uniqueness within the energy class.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文在非散度型线性椭圆方程中，仅假设系数平稳、对称、有限依赖范围及确定性上椭圆界，不要求确定性正下界；在最小特征值倒数具有 $d$ 阶有限矩及 Armstrong–Smart 连续性条件下，建立了定量均匀化估计、二阶大尺度正则性与 Liouville 定理，并刻画了有效矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典均匀化理论通常依赖一致椭圆性（即确定性正下界），但在许多随机介质模型中系数可能退化或仅在随机意义下可控。本文旨在放宽这一限制，在缺乏确定性正下界的情形下建立定量均匀化理论，从而覆盖更一般的退化随机系数场。

Result: 获得了有限胞元误差与含非零源项 Dirichlet 均匀化误差的代数概率界；构造了二次校正子并证明了一阶、二阶大尺度正则性及相应 Liouville 定理；通过不变密度识别有效矩阵，并对密度与加权系数的光滑空间平均给出定量刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作将非散度型方程的定量均匀化推广到无确定性正下界的退化随机系数情形，其应用包括加权梯度收敛以及有效矩阵的有限区域近似。方法上时钟分离与 Green 函数反向 Hölder 估计是控制退化的关键技术工具。

Abstract: We prove quantitative homogenization estimates for linear elliptic equations in nondivergence form with stationary, symmetric coefficients, finite range of dependence, and a deterministic upper ellipticity bound. No deterministic positive lower bound is imposed. We assume that the reciprocal of the infimum of the smallest eigenvalue on a unit ball has a finite moment of order $d$, together with the common continuity condition of Armstrong and Smart. Under these assumptions, we obtain algebraic probability bounds for finite-cell errors and for Dirichlet homogenization errors with nonzero sources. We construct quadratic correctors on the whole space, modulo affine functions, and prove first- and second-order large-scale regularity and the corresponding Liouville theorems. We also identify the effective matrix through the invariant density and quantify smooth spatial averages of the density and the weighted coefficients. The proof separates a unit-trace diffusion from its physical clock. A reverse Hölder estimate for the Green function of a stopped coarse process yields the integrability gain needed to control the clock at the critical moment. Applications include weighted gradient convergence and a finite-domain approximation of the effective matrix.

</details>

Abstract: This paper studies the Cauchy problem for the stochastic Landau--Lifshitz--Baryakhtar equation on $\mathbb{R}^d$, $d=1,2,3$, subject to Stratonovich Gaussian perturbations. We establish a low-regularity well-posedness theory for initial data in $\mathbb{L}^2$, $\mathbb{H}^1$, and $\mathbb{H}^2$. For $\mathbb{L}^2$ initial data, we prove the existence and pathwise uniqueness of global very weak solutions in dimensions one and two, while in dimension three we construct martingale very weak solutions on every finite time interval. For arbitrary $\mathbb{H}^1$ initial data, we prove the existence and uniqueness of global pathwise weak solutions in all dimensions $d\leq3$. Furthermore, for $\mathbb{H}^2$ initial data, we establish the existence and uniqueness of global pathwise strong solutions in the same range of dimensions. The analysis is based on a frequency-truncation approximation scheme combined with stochastic compactness arguments in the whole-space setting. The key step in global continuation is to justify the stochastic effective-field balance at $\mathbb{H}^1$ regularity, where the drift is available only in a negative Sobolev space. Spatial mollification and passage to the limit yield the energy identity for local weak solutions. A coercive modification then gives global energy moments and excludes finite-time blow-up.

Finally, by proving the pathwise uniqueness and normalizing the solutions to the linear equation, we are able to prove the well-posedness of solutions to the required class of nonlinear stochastic equations.

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a viscous regularization for the BGK model of the Boltzmann equation and its hydrodynamic limit. First, we establish rigorous convergence toward the viscous Euler equations, by exploiting bounds derived from spatial Fisher information. We then analyze the formal derivation of the Euler equations with an entropy inequality for both single-and multi-species case, comparing our results against symmetric hyperbolic thermodynamically compatible theory.

Abstract: We study the global dynamics of the generalized Boussinesq equation near standing solitary waves. For even-odd perturbations, Maulen [J. Math. Pures Appl. 177 (2023)] constructed an asymptotically stable center-stable manifold M near the solitary wave Q. We complete this local description by proving that any sufficiently small even-odd perturbation of Q lying outside M either scatters in the energy space as t -> +infinity or blows up in finite time. The proof relies on two main ingredients. First, we classify even-odd solutions with energy below the ground-state energy by identifying two invariant regions, associated with scattering and blow-up, respectively. Second, we establish a one-pass theorem in one dimension: a non-scattering solution cannot re-enter a suitably chosen neighborhood of the solitary wave once it has left that neighborhood. Consequently, the solution remains confined to one of the two invariant regions. Together with the classification above, this yields a dichotomy between scattering and blow-up outside M.

</details>

Abstract: We study bistable pulsating waves for reaction-diffusion equations with general periodic advection in arbitrary space dimension, allowing the diffusion matrix to be nonsymmetric. Assuming that the homogenized equation admits a traveling wave with nonzero speed in a given direction, we construct moving pulsating waves for all sufficiently small spatial periods $L$ and prove their convergence to the homogenized wave as $L\to0^+$. The existence range and convergence are uniform in the propagation direction when the homogenized speeds never vanish. We also prove uniqueness of the wave speed for arbitrary periods, profile uniqueness for moving waves, and stationary-wave uniqueness in the standing case under continuity of the competing profile. For spatially homogeneous reactions, we derive the expansion $c_L=c_0+Lc_1+O(L^2)$ and an explicit formula for $c_1$. Examples with constant diffusion and zero-mean periodic advection show that $c_1$ can have either sign, demonstrating that the heterogeneity in advection may accelerate or decelerate propagation relative to the homogenized limit and that the general $O(L)$ speed estimate is sharp.

</details>

Method: 关键工具包括：(1) 新的模算子理论框架，分离离散与连续谱通道，并通过畸变 Fourier 变换识别辐射剖面；(2) 针对离散孤子剖面建立多个独立刚性准则，从而避免逆散射方法并处理嵌入特征值。此外，框架统一处理全局与有限时间渐近行为。

Conclusion: 论文将孤子分解推广到最优临界正则性，建立了统一的全局与有限时间渐近描述，并给出了精确的质量分配与质量亏损量子化结果。

Abstract: We prove the soliton resolution conjecture for the Calogero--Moser derivative nonlinear Schrödinger equation in the scaling-critical space $L^2_+(\mathbb R)$. For a natural class of initial data including a broad spectral Dini---moment class, global flow--Lax admissible solutions decompose into finitely many explicit modulated solitons plus a dispersive radiation, while finite-time blow-up solutions resolve into quantized zero-carrier $R$-bubbles and a strongly convergent endpoint remainder. This work extends soliton resolution to the optimal critical regularity. Previous results had required weighted $H^{1,1}$ initial data, and in the global case the solution was additionally required to remain in $H^{1,1}$ for all time, which amounts to an extra spatial decay assumption.
  One key ingredient in our argument is a new modular operator-theoretic framework that separates the discrete and continuous spectral channels and identifies the radiation profile through the distorted Fourier transform. A second ingredient is the development of several independent rigidity criteria for the discrete soliton profiles, which avoids inverse scattering and handles embedded eigenvalues. Our framework also yields a unified description of both global and finite-time asymptotics, with exact mass partition and mass-defect quantization.

</details>


### [75] [Stability for coupled second order evolution equations with indirect general memory-dampings without the equal-wave-speeds-type hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39545)
*Kun-Peng Jin,Jin Liang,Ti-Jun Xiao*

Main category: math.AP

Motivation: 已有耦合二阶方程稳定性结果多依赖等波速假设、记忆核非负且递减、或各方程均含阻尼等条件。本文旨在放宽这些限制，处理波速不同、阻尼仅在一个方程、核不必然非负不递减的更一般情形。

Result: 得到能量的最优多项式稳定性结果；该结果涵盖文献中已有的（抽象或具体）二阶耦合方程多项式稳定性结论；并给出抽象结果的具体应用。

Conclusion: 在更弱假设下证明了耦合二阶发展方程组的最优多项式稳定性，推广并统一了相关已有结果。

Abstract: We study the stability for a system of coupled second order evolution equations with indirect general memory-damping without the equal-wave-speeds-type hypothesis in a Hilbert space, where the damping only appears just in one equation, the memory kernel can not necessarily be nonnegative and nonincreasing, and the ``wave speeds" implied by the system can be different. Taking advantage of new processing ideas and with the help of the properties of the Generalized Positive Definite Kernel, we overcome difficulties caused by the different ``wave speeds", the lack of the decreasing and nonnegative property for the memory kernel and the system has only one equation with damping, and obtain an optimal polynomial stability result for the energy, which covers the previous related polynomial stability results for second order coupled equations (abstract or concrete) in the literature. Moreover, applications of the abstract result are given.

</details>

Method: 作者发展了针对GSQG方程修改通量的守恒律估计，并证明了一类新的交换子估计，其界在β→1时一致有界，从而可在β_0附近对参数β进行扰动分析。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了GSQG方程解关于参数β的稳定性，并提供了在β→1时一致有界的交换子估计，这些估计可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: We consider the two-dimensional generalized surface quasi-geostrophic equations in $\mathbb{R}^2$, given by
  \[\partial_t θ+u\cdot \nabla θ=0,\quad u=-\nabla^{\perp}Λ^{β-2}θ,\,β\in [1,2).\] When $β=1$, the equation defines the SQG equation and for $β>1$, it defines a family of more singular active scalar equations. We prove that if the interval of existence of the smooth solution to the generalized SQG equations for some $β_0\in[1,2)$ is $[0,T]$, then with the same initial data, the interval of existence of the generalized SQG equations for $β$ close to $β_0$ also contains $[0,T]$. To prove these results, we develop estimates for a conservation law with flux modified around the generalized SQG equations. Furthermore, we also prove some new commutator estimates with bounds uniformly bounded as $β\to 1$ that may be of independent interest.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该工作把四维正则性结果推广至任意偶数维的尺度不变曲率能量，确立了相应弱临界浸入的解析正则性。

Abstract: We consider scale-invariant curvature energies for immersions of closed manifolds of even dimension $n=2h$ into $\mathbb R^m$, with principal term $\int_Σ \big|\nabla^{(h-1)} \vec{\mathrm{I\!I}}\big|_g^2\,d\text{vol}_g$ and arbitrary lower-order polynomial extrinsic invariants of the same scaling. Following the four-dimensional approach developed in joint work with Bernard, Martino, and Rivière, we prove that every weak critical immersion in the natural Sobolev class $W^{h+1,2}$, whose induced metric and its inverse have $L^\infty$ coefficients, is real-analytic in harmonic coordinates. The proof combines geometric conservation laws, additional structural identities, and elliptic estimates with critical Sobolev coefficients to obtain Morrey decay and bootstrap to full regularity.

### [78] [Explicit Robin Green's Functions, Resonance Spectra, and Impedance Recovery on Annuli and Spherical Shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39643)

TL;DR: 该文构造了环域与球壳域上Helmholtz方程在内外边界具两个独立Robin阻抗时的显式闭式Green函数，揭示共振谱由双线性特征行列式支配，并由此给出逆问题可解性、谱单调性与高频渐近等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环形与球壳域上带双Robin边界条件的Helmholtz问题缺乏显式闭式Green函数与系统的谱理论；同时双阻抗反演的可辨识性与唯一性亟待澄清。

Method: 用Graf加法定理与超球加法定理将各角向模态约化为显式2×2线性系统，得到特征行列式；对双线性结构作代数分析（二次方程反演、反射对称障碍），并对分支作单调性与渐近展开（四角一阶渐近、Dirichlet–Dirichlet角混合二阶系数、低频无共振带与阈值展开、高频间距律及壳曲率修正）；辅以基于Jacobian的灵敏度与条件数准则及数值验证。

Abstract: This paper constructs explicit closed-form Green's functions for the Helmholtz equation on annular and spherical-shell domains with two independent Robin impedances on the inner and outer boundaries. Graf's and the hyperspherical addition theorems reduce each angular mode to an explicit $2\times2$ linear system, and the resonance spectrum is governed by a characteristic determinant bilinear in the two impedances. This bilinearity has a direct inverse-problem consequence: two resonant frequencies of one non-radial angular mode generate at most two candidate impedance pairs via an explicit quadratic equation, a third resonance selects the physical pair, and an exact reflection-symmetry obstruction identifies where the radial spherical mode cannot recover the impedances. Spectrally, we prove the branches positive, simple and strictly increasing in both impedances; derive first-order asymptotics at the four corners of the impedance plane, with coefficients given by boundary masses and normal derivatives of the limiting eigenfunctions, and an explicit mixed second-order coefficient at the Dirichlet--Dirichlet corner, which for the radial mode evaluates in closed form to $2π/(R_2-R_1)^3$; establish a low-frequency resonance-free band, with a second-order threshold expansion explicit in dimension three and a rational approximation accurate over the whole impedance range; and prove the universal high-frequency spacing law with a shell-curvature correction. Jacobian-based sensitivity and conditioning criteria are included. The kernels and spectra are computable to machine precision, all asymptotic regimes are confirmed numerically, and the kernels provide reference solutions for finite-element and boundary-element validation.

</details>

Abstract: We study the reversible reaction $1+2\rightleftarrows3+4$ in a dilute gas described by a Boltzmann system. We prove that the outgoing flux of one product species determines the forward collision kernel throughout the domain on a prescribed admissible set of relative velocities and outgoing directions. The elastic collision laws are known, and the reverse kernel is fixed by a prescribed reciprocity relation. The measurements use two synchronized incoming packets with fixed relative velocity and a common velocity tending to infinity. Under bounded angularly integrated collision rates, the short transit time allows us to isolate the contribution of one forward reaction, with estimates uniform as the packets concentrate. Abstract: In this paper, we establish symmetry results for solutions of overdetermined problems arising in optimal transportation. These problems involve the Monge-Ampère equation with a Dirichlet boundary condition $u=0$ on $\partial Ω$ and the natural boundary condition $Du(Ω)=Ω^{*}$. We show that symmetry holds when the target is the unit ball $B$. For more general target domains, including the cases $Ω^*=Ω$ and arbitrary $Ω^{*}$, symmetry is retained under an additional volume constraint on $Ω$ and a boundary condition on $|Du|$. Finally, we introduce a curvature-type overdetermined problem for the Monge-Ampère equation and obtain ellipsoidal or spherical symmetry under an additional integral normalization condition. Our proofs rely on distinct techniques in different contexts: optimal transport and convex analysis for some cases, integral identities and isoperimetric inequalities for others, and P-function methods for the remaining cases. As a byproduct, in the $τ=2$ case, a new maximum principle for the $P$-function $φ(x)=\sum_{k,l=1}^{n}{\frac{\partial{S_τ(D^2{u})}}{\partial{u_{kl}}}u_{k}u_{l}}-2\binom{n-1}{τ-1}\int_{0}^{u}{f^{\fracτ{n}}(t)\,dt}$ is established for arbitrary positive and nondecreasing $f$, which is of independent interest.

</details>

Abstract: We consider a strongly dispersive Boussinesq-type system arising as a model for the propagation of weakly nonlinear water waves. The system is characterized by a nonlinearity parameter $0<ε\le 1$ and a shallow water parameter $0<μ\le 1$. In the Boussinesq regime $μ\sim ε$, Saut, Wang and Xu \cite{SWX2017} used a hyperbolic approach to prove the well-posedness of the system on a time scale of order $1/ε$, in both one and two dimensions, under a non-cavitation condition. In this paper, we establish well-posedness on a time scale of order $ ( μ^{ 1/4} ε^{-1} h_0 )^{4/3}$ in one dimension and of order $(μ^{ 1/4}ε^{-1} h_0)^{2-}$ in two dimensions, assuming a non-cavitation condition with a parameter $h_0>0$. Our results show quantitatively how the lifespan depends on the balance between nonlinearity and dispersion in the regimes $ε\lesssim μ$ and $μ\llε$. They also make explicit how the existence time deteriorates as $h_0\to0$, that is, as the wave approaches cavitation. Our proofs combine dispersive and Strichartz estimates with energy estimates.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关注两相系统中 Navier–Stokes 相（粘性）与 Euler 相（无粘）耦合时的低马赫数极限。核心困难在于耗散退化：粘性仅作用于 Navier–Stokes 相，Euler 相本身无粘性耗散，需理解拖曳耦合/松弛机制如何传递耗散，并在压力按 ε^{-2} 强奇异缩放时获得对 ε 一致的估计与极限收敛。

Abstract: We study the simultaneous low Mach number limit of a compressible Navier--Stokes--Euler two-phase system in $\mathbb R^3$, in which the pressure terms in both phases are scaled by $\varepsilon^{-2}$. For sufficiently small $H^3$-perturbations around the constant equilibrium, we establish the global well-posedness of the scaled compressible system and derive global energy-dissipation estimates that are uniform with respect to the Mach number $\varepsilon$. A key feature of the analysis is the degenerate dissipation structure: viscosity acts only on the Navier--Stokes phase, while the drag coupling transfers dissipation to the inviscid Euler phase through the relaxation mode. We then prove the global well-posedness and large-time decay of the limiting incompressible two-phase system, and show that the relative velocity $u-ω$ decays faster than the full velocity pair. Finally, for the well-prepared initial data, we introduce pressure-corrected acoustic variables to remove the singular pressure mismatch and establish a global-in-time $H^2$-error. As a result, the scaled compressible solutions converge uniformly in time to the corresponding solution of the limiting incompressible system.

</details>

Result: 借助有限差分格式，证明了正则化系统的适定性与正则性；证明了当正则化参数趋于零时，原模型存在弱解/分布解。该框架将有界区域与含跳间断的非光滑交互核纳入分布解的动理学分割模型理论中。

Abstract: In this article, we analyze a recently introduced kinetic model based on Hegselmann-Krause-type interaction dynamics describing consensus-based image segmentation in the mean-field regime, where particle density depends on spatial coordinates and the normalized gray level (feature) value. The model is formulated as an evolutive nonlocal partial differential equation featuring a non-local spatial drift term involving a bounded confidence interaction kernel, depending on spatial and feature proximity, and a spatial diffusion term degenerating in the feature variable representing aleatoric uncertainties. Establishing well-posedness on a bounded domain, essential for practical image processing, presents significant analytical challenges: the lack of uniform control of space derivatives due to kernels with jump discontinuities and degenerate spatial diffusion, complex non-standard boundary terms under homogeneous Neumann conditions, and a lack of compactness arising from the absence of derivatives with respect to the feature variable. To address these difficulties, we introduce a three-level regularization scheme. Specifically, we smooth the interaction kernel, apply structural boundary regularization to the non-local interaction term to simplify boundary conditions, and incorporate artificial diffusion in spatial and feature variables to resolve degeneracy. We prove the well-posedness and regularity of the regularized system via a finite difference scheme and study the asymptotic limit as regularization parameters vanish, proving the existence of weak/distributional solutions of the original model. This approach advances the mathematical theory of kinetic models for image segmentation by extending the framework of distributional solutions to bounded domains and non-smooth interaction kernels with jump discontinuities.

</details>

Abstract: We study the inversion of the geodesic ray transform from finitely many local averages of its data. Under suitable geometric assumptions, we prove Lipschitz stability on any fixed finite-dimensional reconstruction space when the measurement partition is sufficiently fine. We develop convergent reconstruction algorithms based on Steepest Gradient Descent and Conjugate Gradients and implement them using piecewise constant finite element spaces. Numerical experiments in 2D and 3D illustrate the influence of geometry and measurement discretization on reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [86] [Kerr stability in the full subextremal range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40068)
*Jérémie Szeftel*

Main category: math.AP

Abstract: In the present paper, we extend the results in the sequence of works \cite{KS-GCM1} \cite{KS-GCM2} \cite{KS:Kerr} by Sergiu Klainerman and the author, \cite{GKS22} by Elena Giorgi, Sergiu Klainerman and the author, and \cite{Shen} by Dawei Shen from the slowly rotating regime $|a|\ll m$ to the full subextremal range $|a|<m$, thereby completing the proof of the Kerr stability conjecture. The restriction $|a|\ll m$ enters in fact only through the estimates for wave equations and for the Bianchi system established in \cite{GKS22}. To remove this restriction, we crucially rely on the two companion papers \cite{MaSz24} \cite{MaSz26} by Siyuan Ma and the author, in which we prove energy-Morawetz estimates respectively for the inhomogeneous scalar wave equation and for inhomogeneous Teukolsky equations on perturbations of Kerr with $|a|<m$. Additionally, some of the results of the present paper are proved in our companion paper \cite{Sze}.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 成功构造了Neumann问题在掠射附近的局部时间参数化。Green函数的固定时间衰减率相对无边界情形仍损失1/4，该损失与波前集中swallowtail型奇性相关，且为最优；相应的Strichartz估计通过平衡给定入射角下有损的长时间估计与无损的短时间估计得到，当d=3时平均衰减损失仅为1/6。

Conclusion: Neumann边界条件下掠射波方程的Melrose-Taylor型参数化可以成功构造，其色散衰减损失与Dirichlet情形一致（1/4），且Strichartz估计的最优损失在三维时平均为1/6。

Abstract: We consider the wave equation on a manifold of dimension d>1 with smooth strictly convex boundary, with Neumann boundary condition. We construct a sharp local in time parametrix for the Neumann wave equation near glancing, extending the classical Melrose-Taylor construction for the Dirichlet problem to the Neumann boundary condition. Our construction is based on the microlocal framework and the parametrix developed in [10] for the Dirichlet problem, together with the Melrose-Taylor argument for solving the transport equations under more general, affine-type boundary conditions. Once the Neumann parametrix is constructed, the dispersive and Strichartz estimates follow the analysis developed in [10] for the Dirichlet problem. We therefore only recall the main ingredients of that argument. In particular, the fixed time decay rate for the Green function exhibits the same loss of 1/4 with respect to the boundaryless case, associated with swallowtail type singularities in the wave front set, and this decay is optimal. Moreover, the corresponding Strichartz estimates are obtained by balancing lossy long time estimates at a given incidence with short time ones with no loss: for d=3, this heuristically means that, on average, the decay loss is only 1/6 .

</details>


### [88] [Topological invariants for anisotropic quasilinear elliptic systems: A Poincaré-Hopf formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40145)
*Natalino Borgia*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究乘积空间上含凸梯度项与参数依赖非线性项的泛函，证明其导算子具有(S)_+性质，并由此得到孤立临界点的有限型临界群与Poincaré-Hopf公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对定义在W_0^{1,p}×W_0^{1,q}上的耦合变分泛函，需刻画其临界点的拓扑性质。非线性项H可具次临界与临界增长且依赖参数δ，此类问题缺乏对导算子(S)_+性质及临界群结构的系统分析。

Method: 在凸函数Ψ_1,Ψ_2满足1<p,q<N下的适当条件下，考察泛函I_{δ,Ψ_1,Ψ_2}。利用乘积空间X上的范数与凸性假设，验证Fréchet导数I'_{δ,Ψ_1,Ψ_2}满足(S)_+条件，进而应用临界点理论与Morse理论工具。

Result: 证明了I_{δ,Ψ_1,Ψ_2}'属于(S)_+类；据此推出I_{δ,Ψ_1,Ψ_2}的每个孤立临界点具有有限型临界群，且相应的Poincaré-Hopf公式成立。

Conclusion: 该泛函具有良好变分性质，其孤立临界点的临界群有限型与Poincaré-Hopf公式为后续多重解存在性与指标计算提供了拓扑基础。

Abstract: We consider the functional $I_{δ,Ψ_1,Ψ_2}:X \to \mathbb{R}$ defined for any $z=(u,v) \in X$ as \begin{align*}
  I_{δ,Ψ_1,Ψ_2}(z)
  & = \int_Ω Ψ_1(\nabla u) \, dx + \int_Ω Ψ_2(\nabla v ) \, dx
  & - \int_Ω
  H(δ,x,u(x),v(x)) \,dx, \end{align*} where $Ω$ is a smooth bounded domain of $\mathbb{R}^N$, $Ψ_1, Ψ_2: \mathbb{R}^N \to \mathbb{R}$ are convex functions satisfying suitable conditions for $1 < p, q < N$, and the nonlinearity $H$ is allowed to exhibit both subcritical and critical growth and may depend on a parameter $δ\in I \subseteq \mathbb{R}$. Here, the functional space setting is given by the product space $X :=W_0^{1,p}(Ω)\times W_0^{1,q}(Ω)$ equipped with the norm $\|z\|= \|u\|_{1,p} + \|v\|_{1,q}$ for any $z=(u,v)\in X$, where $\| \cdot \|_{1,s}$ is the usual norm in $W^{1,s}_0(Ω)$. In this paper we prove that $I_{δ,Ψ_1,Ψ_2}'$ is of class $(S)_+$ and we infer that each isolated critical point of $I_{δ,Ψ_1,Ψ_2}$ has critical
  groups of finite type and a Poincaré-Hopf formula holds.

</details>


### [89] [Hadamard Local Well-Posedness for Compressible Liquids with a Free Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40163)
*Tao Luo,Ruixi Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了三维无表面张力自由面液体可压缩Euler方程在Hadamard意义下的局部适定性。在Taylor符号条件与边界相容性条件下，对任意s>3建立了混合Sobolev类中的存在性、唯一性与强连续依赖性：速度与界面具H^s正则性，而焓与速度散度分别属H^{s+1/2}与H^{s-1/2}。证明采用正则化算子光滑运动区域、延拓非线性相容性层级，并结合不同区域解的L^2与部分H^2距离估计；正则解由正则化前向Euler格式构造，高阶能量估计与频率包络给出光滑逼近的强收敛及数据到解映射的连续性，整数Sobolev指标用Lions–Magenes端点相容性条件处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对三维自由面可压缩Euler方程（无表面张力）的适定性研究，此前工作多限于较低正则性或不同函数空间设定。本文旨在在更精细的混合Sobolev类中建立Hadamard意义下的局部适定性，刻画声学方程与自由面、涡量动力学耦合所导致的不同分量正则性差异，并处理整数Sobolev指标的端点情形。

Method: 采用正则化算子对运动区域进行光滑化并延拓非线性相容性层级；建立不同区域上解的L^2及部分H^2距离估计；用正则化前向Euler格式构造正则解；借助高阶能量估计与频率包络获得光滑逼近的强收敛性与数据到解映射的连续性；整数Sobolev指标通过Lions–Magenes端点相容性条件处理。前提为Taylor符号条件与边界相容性条件。

Result: 在Taylor符号条件与边界相容性条件下，对任意s>3于混合Sobolev类中证明了三维无表面张力自由面液体可压缩Euler方程的局部适定性，包括存在性、唯一性与强连续依赖性；速度与界面具H^s正则性，焓属H^{s+1/2}，速度散度属H^{s-1/2}。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了非退化声学方程与自由面、涡量动力学耦合所致的正则性分层，建立了混合Sobolev框架下Hadamard适定性，并为整数Sobolev指标情形提供了基于Lions–Magenes端点相容性条件的处理途径。

Abstract: We prove local well-posedness in the Hadamard sense for the three-dimensional compressible Euler equations governing a liquid with a free surface and no surface tension. Under the Taylor sign condition and the boundary compatibility conditions, we establish existence, uniqueness, and strong continuous dependence in a hybrid Sobolev class for every $s>3$. The velocity and interface have $H^s$ regularity, while the enthalpy and velocity divergence belong to $H^{s+1/2}$ and $H^{s-1/2}$, respectively. This separation reflects the coupling of a nondegenerate acoustic equation with the free-surface and vorticity dynamics. The proof uses regularization operators that smooth the moving domain and extend the nonlinear compatibility hierarchy, together with $L^2$ and partial $H^2$ distance estimates for solutions on different domains. Regular solutions are constructed by a regularized forward Euler scheme. High-order energy estimates and frequency envelopes then yield strong convergence of smooth approximations and continuity of the data-to-solution map. Integer Sobolev indices are treated using a Lions--Magenes endpoint compatibility condition.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在扰动Kerr、全次极端范围|a|<m的框架下，推导Teukolsky波-输运系统，并建立Teukolsky方程的能量-Morawetz估计，同时依托作者与Siyuan Ma合作的两篇论文中关于扰动Kerr上标量波动方程与Teukolsky方程的能量-Morawetz估计。

Result: 给出了\cite{Sze}中两条结果（扰动Kerr中Teukolsky波-输运系统的推导、该设定下Teukolsky的能量-Morawetz估计）的完整证明，从而补全了Kerr稳定性猜想的证明链条。

Conclusion: 本配套论文完善了\cite{Sze}的证明，配合\cite{MaSz24}\cite{MaSz26}，最终支撑了全次极端范围|a|<m下Kerr稳定性猜想的完整证明。

Abstract: The Kerr stability conjecture has been proved in the slowly rotating case, i.e., $|a|\ll m$, in the sequence of works \cite{KS-GCM1} \cite{KS-GCM2} \cite{KS:Kerr} by Sergiu Klainerman and the author, \cite{GKS22} by Elena Giorgi, Sergiu Klainerman and the author, and \cite{Shen} by Dawei Shen, and extended in \cite{Sze}, by the author, to the full subextremal range $|a|<m$, thereby completing the proof of the Kerr stability conjecture. The proof in \cite{Sze} crucially relies on the two companion papers \cite{MaSz24} \cite{MaSz26} by Siyuan Ma and the author, in which we prove energy-Morawetz estimates respectively for the scalar wave equation and for Teukolsky equations on perturbations of Kerr with $|a|<m$. In addition, two results in \cite{Sze}, concerning the derivation of the Teukolsky wave-transport system in perturbations of Kerr and energy-Morawetz estimates for Teukolsky in the setting of \cite{Sze}, are stated without proofs. The goal of the present paper companion paper to \cite{Sze} is to provide the proof of these two results.

</details>

### [91] [UCP Extension in Dimension Three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38539)
*James Tian*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 证明任意有限维算子系统的UCP扩张问题经有限矩阵放大后可归约到三维；矩阵尺寸约为√d，并由此给出Arveson超刚性猜想的（最小）三维反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UCP扩张问题是算子空间与算子代数理论的核心问题，通常直接处理高维算子系统的扩张结构十分复杂。作者希望寻找一个统一、低维的归约框架，从而简化问题并同时检验相关猜想（如Arveson超刚性猜想）的反例存在性。

Method: 对有限维算子系统的UCP扩张问题进行有限矩阵放大，构造保持扩张集合的仿射双射，从而把扩张问题归约到三维情形；并控制所需矩阵尺寸为√d量级（d为原系统维数）。

Result: 每个有限维算子系统的UCP扩张问题都可经矩阵放大归约到三维；扩张集合在该仿射双射下保持不变；矩阵尺寸为O(√d)；由此得到Arveson超刚性猜想的三维反例，并且该反例在维数上是最小的。

Conclusion: 该工作为UCP扩张问题提供了低维归约工具，把高维问题约化到三维并保持扩张结构，同时以最小维数给出Arveson超刚性猜想的反例。

Abstract: We show that every UCP extension problem for a finite dimensional operator system can be reduced to three dimensions after finite matrix amplification. The set of extensions is preserved by an affine bijection. The matrix size is of order $\sqrt{d}$, where $d$ is the dimension of the original system. This also gives a three dimensional (smallest) counterexample to Arveson's hyperrigidity conjecture.

</details>


### [92] [$\mathcal{Z}$-stable and selfless inclusions for the infinite braid group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38596)
*Tron Omland*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文证明了无限辫群包含关系 $C^*_r(P_\infty)\subseteq C^*_r(B_\infty)$ 是 $\mathcal{Z}$-稳定且自失（selfless）的，从而所有中间 $C^*$-代数亦然，给出单生成的非核 $\mathcal{Z}$-稳定简单 $C^*$-代数例子；并证明 $\mathcal{Z}$-稳定性对非内可均群、有限辫群 $B_n$ 及其 Artin 表示的扭叉积失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找自然出现的、单生成的非核 $\mathcal{Z}$-稳定（乃至自失）简单 $C^*$-代数例子，并厘清辫群约化（扭）群 $C^*$-代数在何种条件下满足 $\mathcal{Z}$-稳定性，从而区分内可均性与 $\mathcal{Z}$-稳定性的关系。

Method: 对无限辫群 $B_\infty$ 与纯辫群 $P_\infty$ 的包含关系，使用一个简洁的相对中心化子（relative centralizer）判别准则证明 $\mathcal{Z}$-稳定性；结合自失性的论证推广到所有中间包含与中间 $C^*$-代数，并推广至约化扭群 $C^*$-代数；对反例使用非内可均性及有限 $B_n$、Artin 表示扭叉积的变体论证；方法进一步应用于 $\mathrm{SL}_\infty(\mathbb{Z})$ 等群的递增并。

Result: $C^*_r(P_\infty)\subseteq C^*_r(B_\infty)$ 为 $\mathcal{Z}$-稳定且自失；所有中间包含与中间 $C^*$-代数均 $\mathcal{Z}$-稳定、自失，且为单生成的非核简单 $\mathcal{Z}$-稳定 $C^*$-代数。$C^*_r(\mathbb{F}_\infty)\subseteq C^*_r(B_\infty)$ 为自失包含。所有结果对约化扭群 $C^*$-代数成立。$C^*_r(B_\infty)$ 的自失性已由 Ozawa 的工作得到。反例方面：对所有非内可均群的扭群 $C^*$-代数 $\mathcal{Z}$-稳定性失效；对有限 $B_n$ 的扭群 $C^*$-代数以及 $B_n$ 的 Artin 表示所得扭叉积也失效。最后，方法适用于 $\mathrm{SL}_\infty(\mathbb{Z})$ 等其他群的递增并，其约化群 $C^*$-代数是 $\mathcal{Z}$-稳定的。

Conclusion: 辫群的某些无限包含给出了非核 $\mathcal{Z}$-稳定、自失、单生成的中间 $C^*$-代数自然范例；$\mathcal{Z}$-稳定性与内可均性密切相关（非内可均则失效），而有限辫群情形亦失效；所提相对中心化子方法具有更广的适用性，可处理其他递增并群如 $\mathrm{SL}_\infty(\mathbb{Z})$。

Abstract: Let $B_\infty$ and $P_\infty$ be the braid group and the pure braid group on infinitely many strands. We show that the inclusion $C^*_r(P_\infty)\subseteq C^*_r(B_\infty)$ is $\mathcal{Z}$-stable and selfless. Hence every intermediate inclusion and every intermediate $C^*$-algebra is $\mathcal{Z}$-stable and selfless. The intermediate $C^*$-algebras are natural examples of simple nonnuclear $\mathcal{Z}$-stable $C^*$-algebras, and they are singly generated. The proof of $\mathcal{Z}$-stability uses a simple relative centralizer criterion. We also show that $C^*_r(\mathbb{F}_\infty)\subseteq C^*_r(B_\infty)$ is a selfless inclusion, where $\mathbb{F}_\infty$ is the free group of braids in which only the first string moves. All results hold for reduced twisted group $C^*$-algebras. Selflessness of $C^*_r(B_\infty)$ itself already follows from work of Ozawa. On the other hand, $\mathcal{Z}$-stability fails for all twisted group $C^*$-algebras of groups that are not inner amenable. A variant shows that it fails for all twisted group $C^*$-algebras of $B_n$ with $n$ finite, and for the twisted crossed products from Artin's representation of $B_n$. Finally the method applies to other increasing unions of groups, such as $\mathrm{SL}_\infty(\mathbb{Z})$, whose reduced group $C^*$-algebra is $\mathcal{Z}$-stable.

</details>


### [93] [Central Haagerup Tensor Products and Completely Bounded Maps under Strong Morita Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39241)
*Ilja Gogić*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究强Morita等价C*-代数间的系数作用映射Θ_X的中心Haagerup张量积完备收缩性，将Somerset及Archbold-Somerset-Timoney的定理推广到imprimitivity双模情形，并刻画其单射性、等距性与完全等距性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Somerset与Archbold-Somerset-Timoney先前针对单个C*-代数的中心Haagerup张量积建立了单射性与等距性由Glimm理想的primality刻画的定理。作者希望将这些结果推广到更一般的强Morita等价C*-代数及其imprimitivity双模的框架，探讨系数作用Θ_X的算子空间性质。

Method: 设A与B强Morita等价，由非零imprimitivity双模X={}_AX_B实现，取其标准算子空间结构。通过X典则识别共同乘子中心Z_X，构造系数作用完备收缩Θ_X: A⊗_{Z_X,h}B → CB(X)，其中A⊗_{Z_X,h}B为中心Haagerup张量积。随后分析Θ_X的单射性、等距性与完全等距性，并将逐有限张量长度的范数保持归约到有限ℓ情形，最后通过矩阵稳定化和紧稳定化比较最优常数。

Result: Θ_X为单射当且仅当A的每个Glimm理想是2-primal的；Θ_X为等距（等价地完全等距）当且仅当每个这样的理想是primal的。对每个正整数ℓ，至多ℓ个初等张量之和的范数保持等价于每个Glimm理想的(ℓ²+1)-primality。这些条件同样可施加于B。此外，矩阵稳定化后，用完全有界范数界定中心Haagerup范数的最优常数对X、A和B一致，在每个有限张量长度及完备乘积上成立，并与紧稳定化后的相应常数一致。

Conclusion: 该工作把中心Haagerup张量积的范数性质定理从单个C*-代数推广到强Morita等价对及imprimitivity双模，揭示了单射性和等距性可通过Glimm理想的primality族完整刻画，并证明了稳定化下最优常数的一致性。

Abstract: Let $A$ and $B$ be strongly Morita equivalent $C^*$-algebras, implemented by a nonzero imprimitivity bimodule $X={}_AX_B$ with its canonical operator-space structure. Denote by $Z_X$ their common multiplier centre, canonically identified through $X$. The coefficient action gives a complete contraction $Θ_X:A\otimes_{Z_X,h}B\to\operatorname{CB}(X)$, where $A\otimes_{Z_X,h}B$ is the central Haagerup tensor product obtained by balancing over $Z_X$ and $\operatorname{CB}(X)$ is the space of completely bounded maps on $X$. We show that $Θ_X$ is injective exactly when every Glimm ideal of $A$ is $2$-primal, and isometric, equivalently completely isometric, exactly when every such ideal is primal. For each positive integer $\ell$, norm preservation for sums of at most $\ell$ elementary tensors is equivalent to $(\ell^2+1)$-primality of every Glimm ideal. These conditions may equivalently be imposed on $B$. This extends the corresponding theorems of Somerset and Archbold--Somerset--Timoney to imprimitivity bimodules. We also show that, after matrix stabilization, the optimal constants bounding the central Haagerup norm by the completely bounded norm coincide for $X$, $A$ and $B$, at every finite tensor length and on the completed products, and agree with the corresponding constants after compact stabilization.

</details>


### [94] [Finite free position of maximal abelian $\ast$-subalgebras of the matrix algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39677)
*Yuki Ueda*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究复矩阵代数 $M_n$ 的两个极大交换 $\ast$-子代数何时能处于"有限自由位置"（即其相对位置对任意元素对恰好实现有限自由卷积的 Haar 酉平均），并将此刻画为所有 $r$ 阶子式满足 $|\det U[I,J]|^2=\binom{n}{r}^{-1}$ 的条件；证明该条件成立当且仅当 $n\le3$ 且 $\sqrt{n}U$ 为复 Hadamard 矩阵。为量化 $n\ge4$ 时的偏差，引入均匀子式差异 $\delta_r(U)$，建立其对称性与单调性，并给出平坦酉矩阵下 $\delta_2$ 的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限自由卷积通过对 Haar 酉共轭下的特征多项式取平均得到。作者希望理解：何时两个极大交换 $\ast$-子代数的相对位置能够对每个元素对精确实现这种平均，即何时处于"有限自由位置"。这一问题是自由概率论与矩阵分析交叉处关于有限维精确自由性的基本问题。

Method: 将元素对写成 ${\sf D}_n$ 与 $U{\sf D}_nU^*$（$U$ 为酉矩阵），分别对加性与乘性卷积刻画有限自由位置，条件为对所有 $1\le r\le n$ 及所有 $|I|=|J|=r$ 有 $|\det U[I,J]|^2=\binom{n}{r}^{-1}$。通过复 Hadamard 矩阵刻画该条件，并引入均匀子式差异 $\delta_r(U)$ 来度量 $n\ge4$ 时精确实现的失败，将其与对角矩阵（对角元独立均匀分布于单位圆）的有限自由乘性卷积第 $r$ 项系数的均方误差等同，进而研究其对称性与单调性，并对平坦酉矩阵推导 $\delta_2$ 的显式公式。

Result: 有限自由位置条件成立当且仅当 $n\le3$ 且 $\sqrt{n}U$ 为复 Hadamard 矩阵；对于 $n\ge4$ 精确实现失败。均匀子式差异满足对称性 $\delta_r(U)=\delta_{n-r}(U)$ 与单调性 $\delta_1(U)\le\delta_2(U)\le\cdots\le\delta_{\lfloor n/2\rfloor}(U)$。对平坦酉矩阵，导出 $\delta_2$ 的显式公式，得到 $2\le r\le n-2$ 时 $\delta_r(U)\ge\frac{n-3}{2n}$；当且仅当 $\sqrt{n}U$ 的逐元素平方也是复 Hadamard 矩阵时，$r=2$ 取等号。

Conclusion: 两个极大交换 $\ast$-子代数能精确实现有限自由卷积（有限自由位置）的维数限制极为苛刻，仅 $n\le3$ 且 $\sqrt{n}U$ 为复 Hadamard 矩阵时成立。对于 $n\ge4$，用均匀子式差异 $\delta_r(U)$ 定量刻画偏差，其具有对称性与单调性，并给出平坦酉矩阵情形下的下界与取等条件，揭示了有限自由性与复 Hadamard 结构之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: Finite free convolution is obtained by averaging characteristic polynomials over Haar unitary conjugation. We ask when two maximal abelian $\ast$-subalgebras of the complex matrix algebra ${\sf M}_n$ can be placed in finite free position: that is, when their relative position realizes this averaging exactly for every pair of elements, one from each subalgebra. Writing such a pair as ${\sf D}_n$ and $U{\sf D}_nU^*$ with $U$ unitary, we characterize finite free position, for either additive or multiplicative convolution, by the condition $|\det U[I,J]|^2=\binom{n}{r}^{-1}$ for every $1\le r\le n$ and all $I,J$ with $|I|=|J|=r$. We show that this condition holds if and only if $n\le3$ and $\sqrt{n} U$ is a complex Hadamard matrix.
  To quantify the failure of exact realization for $n\ge 4$, we introduce the uniform-minor discrepancy $δ_r(U)$. We identify it with the mean-square error in the $r$-th coefficient of finite free multiplicative convolution for two diagonal matrices whose diagonal entries are independent and uniformly distributed on the unit circle. We establish the symmetry $δ_r(U)=δ_{n-r}(U)$ and the monotonicity $δ_1(U)\leδ_2(U)\le\cdots\le δ_{\lfloor n/2\rfloor}(U)$. For flat unitaries, we derive an explicit formula for $δ_2$, yielding $δ_r(U)\ge\frac{n-3}{2n}$ for $2\le r\le n-2$. Equality for $r=2$ holds precisely when the entrywise square of $\sqrt{n}U$ is also complex Hadamard.

</details>


### [95] [Dynamical $\mathrm C^*$-algebras and coarse geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39762)
*Bruno de Mendonça Braga*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文从动力系统视角研究一致Roe代数与拟局部代数的关系，用对角流σ_h的连续性/解析性刻画这两个代数，并给出介于二者之间的不变C*-代数谱系，证明该谱系在膨胀图情形下非退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一致Roe代数与拟局部代数均编码度量空间的大尺度几何，二者是否相等是Roe提出的经典问题，近期才由Ozawa解决。作者希望为该问题提供新的动力系统视角并揭示两代数之间的中间结构。

Method: 对集合X，每个映射h: X→ℝ通过对角酉算子e^{ith}的共轭诱导B(ℓ_2(X))上的一参数自同构群σ_h。研究该流的连续性点与解析性，用h诱导的伪度量刻画一致Roe代数；在X为一致局部有限度量空间且h遍历所有粗映射时，给出两代数的动力刻画，并考察介于连续与解析之间的正则性条件所生成的C*-代数。

Result: σ_h的连续性点恰为由h诱导的伪度量的一致Roe代数；C*_ql(X)恰为对所有此类流连续的算子集合，C*_u(X)恰为解析算子的范数极限。介于连续与解析之间的正则性条件产生介于C*_u(X)与C*_ql(X)之间、在双射粗等价下不变的C*-代数谱系；当X为膨胀图的粗不交并时，在每个对角流下于条形区域解析的算子所生成的代数严格介于C*_u(X)与C*_ql(X)之间。

Conclusion: 该工作用对角流的连续性—解析性光谱统一刻画了一致Roe代数与拟局部代数，构建了二者之间的不变C*-代数谱系，并通过膨胀图例子证明该谱系非退化，为Roe问题的解决提供了新的动力学框架。

Abstract: Both the uniform Roe algebras and the quasi-local algebras encode the large scale geometry of metric spaces. Whether these algebras coincide is a question which goes back to Roe and has only been recently solved by Ozawa. We propose a dynamical point of view on this problem. Given a set $X$, every map $h\colon X\to\mathbb{R}$ induces a one-parameter group $σ_h$ of automorphisms of $\mathcal{B}(\ell_2(X))$, given by conjugation by the diagonal unitaries $e^{ith}$, and we show that the operators which are continuity points of $σ_h$ are precisely the uniform Roe algebra of the pseudo-metric induced by $h$. If we moreover assume that $X$ is a uniformly locally finite metric space and $h$ is allowed to range over all coarse maps, this gives dynamical characterizations of both algebras: $\mathrm{C}^*_{ql}(X)$ consists exactly of the operators which are continuous for all such flows, while $\mathrm{C}^*_u(X)$ consists exactly of norm limits of those which are analytic. The regularity conditions lying between continuity and analyticity then give rise to $\mathrm{C}^*$-algebras between $\mathrm{C}^*_u(X)$ and $\mathrm{C}^*_{ql}(X)$ which are invariant under bijective coarse equivalence, and we show that this scale is not degenerate: if $X$ is a coarse disjoint union of expander graphs, the algebra generated by operators which are analytic on a strip for every diagonal flow sits strictly between $\mathrm{C}^*_u(X)$ and $\mathrm{C}^*_{ql}(X)$.

</details>


### [96] [K-theoretic invariants of the Toeplitz operator system and its dual, and of all graph operator systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40028)
*Walter D. van Suijlekom*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文计算了若干有限维算子系统的 K-理论不变量，包括 Toeplitz 算子系统及其对偶（Fejér-Riesz 算子系统）以及与容差关系相关的图算子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望理解有限维算子系统的 K-理论不变量，并考察这些不变量是否与其 C*-包络的相应不变量一致，从而揭示算子系统与 C*-代数在 K-理论层面的异同。

Method: 针对不同情形采用多种技巧：推广块 Toeplitz 矩阵的 Carathéodory 分解结果、Wiener-Hopf 分解以及 Schur 补方法。

Result: 在大多数情形下，所计算的 K-理论不变量与其 C*-包络的类似不变量相吻合；例外是 Fejér-Riesz 算子系统的 K1-不变量。

Conclusion: 研究表明有限维算子系统的 K-理论不变量在多数情况下可由其 C*-包络决定，但 Fejér-Riesz 算子系统的 K1-不变量构成显著例外，说明算子系统层面可能存在 C*-包络无法捕捉的 K-理论信息。

Abstract: We compute $K$-theoretic invariants for several finite-dimensional operator systems. These include the Toeplitz operator system and its dual (the Fejér-Riesz operator system), as well as operator systems associated to tolerance relations, aka graph operator systems. A variety of techniques is applied, adapted to each of these cases, ranging from a generalization of Carathéodory's factorization result for block Toeplitz matrices, to Wiener-Hopf factorization and Schur complements. In most instances we find that the invariants coincide with their analogues for the $C^*$-envelope, except for the $K_1$-invariants of the Fejér-Riesz operator system.

</details>


### [97] [K-theoretic invariants of four three-dimensional operator systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40029)
*Walter D. van Suijlekom*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文计算四个三维算子系统的K理论不变量以例示一般理论，发现这些不变量在部分情形下可区分算子系统与其C*-包络，部分情形下则不能，并探讨理论的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过具体例子说明算子系统K理论不变量的行为，考察其区分子系统与C*-包络的能力。

Method: 对四个三维算子系统进行K理论不变量的具体计算。

Result: 在部分情形下，不变量能够区分算子系统与其C*-包络；在另一些情形下则无法区分。

Conclusion: 该K理论不变量对算子系统的区分能力依赖于具体系统，并讨论了该理论的可能推广。

Abstract: We compute $K$-theoretic invariants for four three-dimensional operator systems to illustrate the general theory. We find that in some cases the invariants distinguish the operator system from its $C^*$-envelope, while in others they do not. We also explore extensions of the theory.

</details>


### [98] [Convergence of spectral smoothings from compact quantum group actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40189)
*Trevor Camper,Dimitrios Giannakis,Gage Hoefer*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究由紧量子群作用于C*-代数产生的"光滑化"算子系统在完全与量子Gromov-Hausdorff距离下的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前利用光滑化逼近C*-代数的工作（如谱截断）多针对特定情形，缺少由紧量子群作用统一刻画的框架，本文旨在给出一般性构造与收敛定理。

Method: 设共同可均（co-amenable）紧量子群𝔾作用于含单位C*-代数𝒜，且二者配备合适的量子度量；利用L¹(𝔾)中的有界近似单位构造𝒜的逼近算子系统，并在完全与量子Gromov-Hausdorff距离下证明收敛。

Result: 证明了L¹(𝔾)中的有界近似单位可用于在完全与量子Gromov-Hausdorff距离下逼近原空间𝒜。

Conclusion: 作为应用，给出了紧群谱截断收敛性的结果，并与已有结论建立了联系。

Abstract: In this note, we study complete and quantum Gromov-Hausdorff convergence of operator systems obtained using smoothings of C*-algebras, where our smoothings arise from the action of a compact quantum group on the C*-algebra. Specifically, we show how if $\mathbb{G}$ is a co-amenable compact quantum group acting on unital C*-algebra $\mathcal{A}$, and both are equipped with suitable quantum metrics, bounded approximate identities in $L^{1}(\mathbb{G})$ may be used for approximations of the original space $\mathcal{A}$ under the complete and quantum Gromov-Hausdorff distance. We finish by presenting some applications to the convergence of spectral truncations of compact groups and connections to previous results.

</details>


### [99] [Connes-embeddability and twisted group von Neumann algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40246)
*Soham Chakraborty,Felipe Flores*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文引入"cocycle-hyperlinear群"概念，即所有扭曲群冯诺依曼代数均为Connes-可嵌入的可数离散群，并研究其包含的群类及封闭性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将超线性（hyperlinear）群的概念推广到扭曲情形，以刻画群冯诺依曼代数的Connes-可嵌入性。

Method: 定义cocycle-hyperlinear群，并通过对具体群类（树able群的amenable扩张、SL(n,Z)、GL(n,Z)、PSL(n,Z)的中心扩张）进行构造性验证，以及证明该类在多种群运算下的封闭性。

Result: cocycle-hyperlinear群类包含树able群被amenable群的扩张，以及SL(n,Z)、GL(n,Z)、PSL(n,Z)（n>1）的中心扩张；该类对直积、自由积、amenable融合的融合自由积、amenable群的HNN扩张，以及某些商、扩张和超群运算封闭。

Conclusion: cocycle-hyperlinear群构成一个在多种构造下封闭的广泛群类，为研究扭曲群冯诺依曼代数的Connes-可嵌入性提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We call a countable discrete group cocycle-hyperlinear if all its twisted group von Neumann algebras are Connes-embeddable. We show that the class of cocycle-hyperlinear groups includes extensions of treeable groups by amenable groups and central extensions of SL(n,Z), GL(n,Z) and PSL(n,Z) for n greater than 1. We also show that this class is closed under taking direct products, free products, amalgamated free products with amenable amalgams, HNN extensions by amenable groups, as well as certain quotients, extensions, and overgroups.

</details>

### [100] [Kadison-Kastler Distance and Interior Angle for Masas: Connections with Entropy and Probabilistic Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38206)
*Indrajit Ghosh,Sumit Kumar*
