Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究有限维矩阵代数中masa（极大阿贝尔自伴子代数）之间的Kadison–Kastler距离与内角，在M_2(C)中给出KK距离的显式公式，证明[0,1]中每个值均可取到；在一般M_n(C)中证明内角的酉不变性并给出任意masa间夹角的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望刻画有限维矩阵代数中masa之间的Kadison–Kastler距离与内角，并揭示其与最大距离、最大内角、最大Connes–Størmer修正熵、最小Popa概率指标之间的等价关系。

Method: 在M_2(C)中推导KK距离的显式公式，利用相对酉元与Hadamard酉元的关系；对一般M_n(C)，证明内角的酉不变性并推导任意masa uΔ^{(n)}u^*与vΔ^{(n)}v^*间夹角的显式公式。

Result: M_2(C)中KK距离在[0,1]上取满，最大值当且仅当相对酉元为Hadamard酉元；群胚正规化子中元素对应的距离取最小或最大值；对中间子代数有d_KK(A,B)=sinα(A,B)；并证明d_KK=1、h=log2、α=π/2、λ=1/2四者等价。M_n(C)中内角为π/2当且仅当相对酉元u^*v为Hadamard酉元，且[0,π/2]中每个值均可取到。

Conclusion: 有限维矩阵代数中masa的KK距离与内角、修正熵、概率指标之间存在精确对应关系，Hadamard酉元刻画了极值情形，且距离与角度的取值范围分别为[0,1]与[0,π/2]。

Abstract: We study the Kadison--Kastler distance and the interior angle between masas in finite-dimensional matrix algebras. In \(\mathbb{M}_2(\mathbb{C})\), we obtain an explicit formula for the Kadison--Kastler distance between two masas and show that every value in \([0,1]\) is attained by \(\mathrm{d}_{\textrm{KK}}(Δ,uΔu^*)\). We further prove that the distance attains its maximal value precisely when the relative unitary is a Hadamard unitary. In addition, for every $v$ in the groupoid normaliser of $Δ$, the Kadison--Kastler distance between \(Δ\) and \(vΔv^*\) assumes either its minimal or maximal possible value. We also establish the identity \[ \mathrm{d}_{\textrm{KK}}(\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B})=\sinα(\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}) \] for any two intermediate subalgebras \(\mathbb{C}\subsetneq\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}\subsetneq\mathbb{M}_2(\mathbb{C})\). Most notably, for any unitaries \(u,v\in\mathbb{M}_2(\mathbb{C})\), we establish the equivalence of maximal Kadison--Kastler distance, maximal interior angle, maximal Connes--Størmer modified entropy, and minimal Popa probabilistic index: \[ \mathrm{d}_{\textrm{KK}}(uΔu^*,vΔv^*)=1 \iff h(uΔu^*,vΔv^*)=\log 2 \iff α(uΔu^*,vΔv^*)=\fracπ{2} \iff λ(uΔu^*,vΔv^*)=\frac{1}{2}. \] For general \(\mathbb{M}_n(\mathbb{C})\), we prove a unitary invariance property for the interior angle and derive an explicit formula for the angle between arbitrary masas of the form \(uΔ^{(n)}u^*\) and \(vΔ^{(n)}v^*\). As a consequence, the interior angle is \(\fracπ{2}\) precisely when the relative unitary \(u^*v\) is a Hadamard unitary. Finally, we show that every value in \(\left[0,\fracπ{2}\right]\) is attained as the interior angle between a pair of masas in \(\mathbb{M}_n(\mathbb{C})\).

</details>


### [101] [Zong's problem on $2d$ half-spaces containing the unit ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38394)
*Grigory Ivanov*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明：在 ℝ^d 中包含欧氏单位球的 2d 个半空间的交集含有一个范数至少为 √d 的点，等号情形刻画为立方体；证明通过高斯正交象限概率不等式及权重视角给出下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究包含单位球的 2d 个半空间（即对应 d 个方向及其反向约束）所构成的多面体，其"最外点"能延伸多远；该量与内接/外切多面体的几何极值、随机高斯权下的顶点平均范数、以及立方体的刻画相关，期望给出精确的最优常数 √d 及等号条件。

Method: 将平均平方范数按高斯测度对顶点法锥加权表示，从而把问题转化为高斯正交象限概率（orthant probabilities）的不等式；该核心不等式通过一维的质量、质心与边界密度比较（one-dimensional comparison of mass, centroid, and boundary density）证明；并讨论其二次型推广及一个独立的谱估计。

Result: 得到范数下界 √d，等号仅由立方体达到；同时获得以顶点法锥高斯测度加权的平均平方范数下界，以及高斯正交象限概率不等式和二次推广与谱估计。

Conclusion: 包含 ℝ^d 单位球的 2d 个半空间交集必含有范数≥√d 的点，且等号唯一由立方体实现；核心技术是高斯正交象限概率不等式，其结果对相关极值问题与谱方法具有独立价值。

Abstract: We prove that the intersection of $2d$ halfspaces containing the Euclidean unit ball in $\mathbb{R}^d$ contains a point of norm at least $\sqrt{d}$. Equality characterizes the cube. The proof gives a lower bound for the average squared norm of the vertices, weighted by the Gaussian measures of their normal cones. The main step is an inequality for Gaussian orthant probabilities, proved by a one-dimensional comparison of mass, centroid, and boundary density. We also discuss a quadratic extension and give an independent spectral estimate.

</details>


### [102] [The Lotz Property in Weakly Compactly Generated Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38396)
*Gustavo Araújo,Mateus Azevedo,Flank Bezerra,Anderson Barbosa*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究无限维弱紧生成（WCG）Banach空间的Lotz性质；证明非可分WCG空间必不具备Lotz性质，而可分WCG空间中两种情况均存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨"是否所有无限维WCG Banach空间都不具备Lotz性质"这一问题，厘清WCG空间与Lotz性质的关系。

Method: 利用WCG空间的经典分解定理构造Schauder分解与具无界生成元的对角C_0半群；建立Calkin代数判据（若X不具备有界紧逼近性质且Cal(X)中每个不可逆元m次幂为零，则X具Lotz性质），结合Maurey–Pisier–Szankowski抽取定理与Argyros–Motakis构造。

Result: 非可分情形下结论成立：任意非可分WCG空间均非Lotz；但无限制断言为假。由Calkin代数判据（适用于每个算子为纯量加严格奇异、且每个严格奇异算子某固定幂紧的情形）构造出可分遗传不可分解Lotz空间：自反例子，以及复域上不含无限维自反子空间的例子；所考虑的Argyros–Motakis空间为Lotz饱和但自身非Lotz。

Conclusion: 每个非可分WCG空间非Lotz，而可分WCG类中同时包含Lotz与非Lotz空间。

Abstract: We study whether every infinite-dimensional weakly compactly generated (WCG) Banach space fails the Lotz property. The answer is affirmative in the nonseparable case: a classical decomposition theorem for WCG spaces provides a Schauder decomposition, and an explicit diagonal $C_0$-semigroup has an unbounded generator. The unrestricted assertion is false. Our main abstract result is a Calkin-algebra criterion: if $X$ fails the bounded compact approximation property and, for some fixed $m$, every noninvertible element of $\Cal(X)=\Lop(X)/\Kop(X)$ has $m$th power zero, then $X$ has the Lotz property. In particular, this applies when every operator is scalar-plus-strictly-singular and one fixed power of every strictly singular operator is compact. Combining this criterion with the Maurey--Pisier--Szankowski extraction theorem and constructions of Argyros and Motakis yields separable hereditarily indecomposable Lotz spaces over both scalar fields: reflexive examples, and, over the complex field, an example containing no infinite-dimensional reflexive subspace. Moreover, the ambient Argyros--Motakis spaces considered here are Lotz-saturated, although they themselves are non-Lotz. Thus every nonseparable WCG space is non-Lotz, while the separable WCG class contains both Lotz and non-Lotz spaces.

</details>


### [103] [The weak maximizing property is not inherited by subspaces of the domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38410)
*Alessandro Costa,Vinícius Miranda,Geivison Ribeiro*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个可分的自反Banach格$X$及其闭子格$E$，使得每个从$X$到$\ell_2$的有界线性算子都是紧的，但$E$上存在不达到范数且具有弱收敛于非零向量的正最大化序列的正算子，从而否定回答了关于弱最大化性质被闭子空间继承的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dantas、Jung和Martínez-Cervantes提出疑问：弱最大化性质（weak maximizing property）是否能被定义域的闭子空间继承。本文旨在通过构造反例来否定地回答这一问题，同时也否定回答正弱最大化性质在自反Banach格的闭子格上的相应问题。

Method: 构造一个可分的自反Banach格$X$及其闭子格$E\subseteq X$，满足每个从$X$到$\ell_2$的有界线性算子都是紧的，但在$E$上存在正算子不达到其范数且拥有弱收敛于非零向量的正最大化序列。进一步利用标准的格复化（canonical lattice complexification）将反例推广到复域。

Result: 否定了Dantas、Jung和Martínez-Cervantes提出的关于闭子空间继承弱最大化性质的问题；同时否定了正弱最大化性质在自反Banach格闭子格上的相应问题；并通过格复化给出了复域上原始问题的反例。

Conclusion: 弱最大化性质（及其正版本）不被定义域的闭子空间（或闭子格）继承，作者通过具体构造的反例证明了这一点，并在实域和复域上均给出了否定答案。

Abstract: We construct a separable reflexive Banach lattice $X$ and a closed sublattice $E\subseteq X$ such that every bounded linear operator from $X$ into $\ell_2$ is compact, whereas there exists a positive operator from $E$ into $\ell_2$ that does not attain its norm and admits a positive maximizing sequence converging weakly to a nonzero vector. This answers negatively the question posed by Dantas, Jung and Martínez-Cervantes in [4] concerning the inheritance of the weak maximizing property by closed subspaces of the domain. It also answers negatively the corresponding question for the positive weak maximizing property and closed sublattices of reflexive Banach lattices. Canonical lattice complexification yields a counterexample to the original question over the complex field as well.

</details>


### [104] [Derivatives of symplectic spectral functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40175)
*Hemant K. Mishra,Niloy Paul,Temjensangba*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究辛谱函数 $f\circ d$ 的可微性与 Clarke 广义梯度，证明其 Fréchet 可微、严格 Fréchet 可微、连续 Gâteaux 可微性均等价于对称函数 $f$ 在 $d(A)$ 处相应性质，并给出 Clarke 广义梯度的刻画；应用于玻色忠实高斯态的纯度、von Neumann 熵和 Rényi 熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辛谱函数由辛特征值的对称函数构成，其可微性与非光滑分析性质此前缺乏系统研究，而量子信息中高斯态的纯度与熵正是协方差矩阵的辛谱函数，因此需要建立其可微性理论与梯度公式。

Method: 以辛特征值向量映射 $d(A)=(d_1(A),\ldots,d_n(A))$ 与对称函数 $f$ 的复合为对象，研究 $f\circ d$ 的 Fréchet 可微性、严格 Fréchet 可微性、连续 Gâteaux 可微性及 Clarke 广义梯度，并将结果用于高斯态协方差矩阵的相关量。

Result: 证明 $f\circ d$ 在 $A$ 处 Fréchet 可微当且仅当 $f$ 在 $d(A)$ 处 Fréchet 可微，并显式给出导数；严格 Fréchet 可微与连续 Gâteaux 可微亦等价；确定 $f\circ d$ 的 Clarke 广义梯度由 $f$ 在 $d(A)$ 处的 Clarke 广义梯度表示；并得到纯度、von Neumann 熵、Rényi 熵作为协方差矩阵函数是 Fréchet 可微的及其显式导数。

Conclusion: 该工作将对称函数的可微性与广义梯度理论推广到辛谱函数，获得等价刻画与导数公式，并成功应用于高斯量子态的纯度与熵，为非光滑量子信息量的分析提供了工具。

Abstract: A symplectic spectral function of a $2n \times 2n$ real positive definite matrix $A$ is a function of its symplectic eigenvalues $0< d_1(A) \leq \cdots \leq d_n(A)$, which is given by a composition $f \circ d$ of some symmetric function $f$ on the set of $n$-vectors with positive entries and the symplectic eigenvalue vector map $d(A)=(d_1(A),\ldots, d_n(A))$. In this work, we rigorously study various types of differentiability and Clarke generalized gradient of symplectic spectral functions, and also provide several applications of our findings. We show that the symplectic spectral function $f \circ d$ is Fréchet differentiable at $A$ if and only if $f$ is Fréchet differentiable at $d(A)$, and we compute the derivative expression explicitly. We also show that $f \circ d$ is strictly Fréchet differentiable (respectively, continuously Gâteaux differentiable) at $A$ if and only if $f$ is strictly Fréchet differentiable (respectively, continuously Gâteaux differentiable) at $d(A)$. We determine the Clarke generalized gradient of a symplectic spectral function $f \circ d$ at $A$, which is given in terms of the Clarke generalized gradient of $f$ at $d(A)$. As an application of our work, we show that the purity, von Neumann entropy, and Rényi entropy of a bosonic faithful Gaussian state are Fréchet differentiable functions of the covariance matrix of the Gaussian state. We also provide explicit expressions of their Fréchet derivatives.

</details>


### [105] [Weak type $(1,1)$ boundedness of noncommutative singular integral operators with rough kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38671)
*Xudong Lai*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文建立了具有粗糙核的非交换奇异积分算子的弱型$(1,1)$有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗糙核情形下非交换奇异积分算子的端点弱型$(1,1)$估计此前尚未解决，其困难在于经典欧氏空间方法难以直接推广到非交换框架。

Method: 针对粗糙核，在好函数的伪局部化论证中发展核本身的某种正交性；对坏函数则采用微局部分解与$TT^*$论证。

Result: 证明了非交换粗糙核奇异积分算子的弱型$(1,1)$有界性。

Conclusion: 该结果将非交换奇异积分理论的端点估计推广至粗糙核情形，所发展的核正交性与微局部分解技术为处理粗糙核提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the weak type $(1,1)$ boundedness of noncommutative singular integral operators with rough kernels. To deal with the rough kernels, we develop some orthogonality of the kernels themselves in the pseudo-localization arguments for the good functions and use the microlocal decomposition and $TT^*$ arguments for the bad functions.

</details>


### [106] [On partition functions of Gaussian random variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38904)
*Omer Friedland,Olivier Guédon,Fabien Souli*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 对中心化高斯向量 $X$（方差为1）定义配分函数 $Z_\beta(X)=\sum_{i=1}^N e^{\beta X_i}$，证明其广义 $L_q$-均值在 $q=1$ 处发生完整相变：$q<1$ 时单位向量正则单纯形配置的协方差矩阵 $\Delta_N$ 使均值最大，$q>1$ 时使其最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机配分函数 $Z_\beta(X)$ 的广义 $L_q$-均值随 $q$ 变化的行为，揭示 $q=1$ 处的相变现象，并刻画最大化/最小化这些均值的协方差结构，从而理解高斯随机场中极值统计与凸性/集中现象的内在机制。

Method: 分两种情形处理：对 $q>1$（$\beta\neq0$），证明矩泛函在整个相关矩阵集合上全局强凸，给出显式的凸性模量与定量的质心-形状稳定性估计；对 $q<1$，证明反 Brascamp--Lieb 型对数凹剖面的普遍比较定理，并特化到 Gumbel 剖面，得到配分函数之间的 Laplace 变换比较。

Result: 确立 $q=1$ 处完整相变：$q<1$ 时正则单纯形协方差 $\Delta_N$ 最大化广义 $L_q$-均值，$q>1$ 时最小化之；两种机制分别由强凸性（$q>1$）与反 Brascamp--Lieb 型比较（$q<1$）支配。

Conclusion: 广义 $L_q$-均值在 $q=1$ 处存在由不同数学原理控制的双相区：凸性主导 $q>1$，对数凹比较主导 $q<1$；正则单纯形配置在两侧分别扮演极值角色，给出配分函数集中性与极值结构的定量刻画。

Abstract: For $β\in \mathbb{R}$, we define the partition function $$ Z_β(X) = \sum_{i=1}^N \exp(βX_i) $$ of a centered Gaussian random vector $X=(X_1, \ldots, X_N)$ with $\mathbb{E}[X_i^2]= 1$. For $q \in \mathbb{R}$, we prove a complete phase transition for the generalized $L_q$-means of $Z_β(X)$ at $q=1$. The centered Gaussian vector with covariance matrix $Δ_N$ obtained from a regular simplex configuration of unit vectors maximizes these means for $q<1$, and minimizes them for $q>1$.
  The two regimes are governed by different principles. For $q > 1$ {and $β\ne0$}, the moment functional is globally strongly convex on the entire set of correlation matrices, with an explicit modulus of convexity and a quantitative centroid-shape stability estimate. For $q<1$, the strategy is different. We prove a universal comparison for log-concave profiles of reverse Brascamp--Lieb type. Specializing this result to the Gumbel profile yields a Laplace-transform comparison between the partition functions.

</details>


### [107] [Morita equivalence for $L^p$-operator algebras associated with étale groupoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39031)
*Yeong Chyuan Chung,Alonso Delfín,Zhen Wang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明等价的局部紧、局部Hausdorff、具有仿紧单位空间的étale群胚，其约化与全L^p算子代数对所有p∈[1,∞]是Morita等价的，并研究约化与全情形下的技术细节、linking代数比较及Morita cycle与若干应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在将群胚算子代数的Morita等价理论从经典的C*-代数（p=2）情形推广到一般的L^p算子代数框架，解决约化范数在1<p<∞时非无条件性所带来的技术困难，并建立相应的Morita理论工具。

Method: 对约化情形引入配对值近似单位以处理约化范数的非无条件性；对全情形通过膨胀空间表示证明全-开闭约化定理；比较约化Morita等价的具体p-linking代数与linking群胚的约化L^p算子代数；研究由proper étale群胚对应产生的Morita cycle（在适当扩张假设下含约化情形）。

Result: 等价群胚的约化与全L^p算子代数是Morita等价的；linking代数的典范比较映射是收缩、单射且具有稠密值域，在p=2时为等距同构；得到Green对称imprimitivity定理的L^p版本、粗群胚与逆半群的结果，以及相应K-理论的 invariance。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了étale群胚L^p算子代数的Morita等价理论，统一并推广了经典结果，为L^p算子代数框架下的Morita理论、K-理论不变性及相关应用提供了基础。

Abstract: We prove that equivalent locally compact, locally Hausdorff, étale groupoids with paracompact unit spaces have Morita equivalent reduced and full $L^p$-operator algebras for every $p\in[1,\infty]$. The reduced theorem requires pairing-valued approximate identities, reflecting the failure of unconditionality of the reduced norm for $1<p<\infty$, while the full theorem rests on a full-clopen reduction theorem proved by dilating spatial representations. For $1\leq p<\infty$, we also compare the concrete $p$-linking algebra of the reduced Morita equivalence with the reduced $L^p$-operator algebra of the linking groupoid: the canonical comparison is contractive, injective, and has dense range in general, and is an isometric isomorphism for $p=2$. We further study Morita cycles arising from proper étale groupoid correspondences, including the reduced case under suitable extension hypotheses. Applications include an $L^p$ version of Green's symmetric imprimitivity theorem, results for coarse groupoids and inverse semigroups, and invariance of the corresponding $K$-theory.

</details>


### [108] [Functional inequalities along Wasserstein geodesics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39252)
*Nathael Gozlan,Hugo Malamut,Irène Waldspurger*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究沿 Wasserstein 测地线的函数不等式。对被 κ₀ 与 κ₁ 强对数凹的两个概率测度，证明其二次 Wasserstein 测地线满足一个锐利的函数不等式，线性化后恢复 Han–Zhu 的 Poincaré 估计；在实轴上证明最优 T₁、T₂ 常数平方根沿单调插值的凸性，并将结果推广到重标定 Lᵖ Poincaré 常数；最后构造端点满足 T₂ 及所有有限 p 的 Lᵖ-Poincaré 不等式、但内部插值均失效的平面 Wasserstein 测地线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画函数不等式（如 Poincaré、T₁/T₂、Lᵖ-Poincaré 等）沿最优传输插值的传递与凸性规律，统一并推广已有 Poincaré 估计，并厘清端点不等式能否保证内点不等式。

Method: 沿 Wasserstein 测地线研究函数不等式；对强对数凹测度间的二次 Wasserstein 测地线建立锐利不等式并作线性化；在实轴上分析最优 T₁、T₂ 常数平方根沿单调插值的凸性，推广至一般传输熵不等式及重标定 Lᵖ Poincaré 常数；构造反例性平面测地线。

Result: 得到强对数凹测度间二次 Wasserstein 测地线的锐利函数不等式，线性化可恢复 Han–Zhu 的 Poincaré 估计；证明实轴上最优 T₁、T₂ 常数平方根沿单调插值的凸性，并推广到一般传输熵不等式与重标定 Lᵖ Poincaré 常数（含 Poincaré 常数平方根与逆 Cheeger 常数）；构造出端点满足 T₂ 及所有有限 p 的 Lᵖ-Poincaré 不等式、但每个内部插值均失败的平面 Wasserstein 测地线。

Conclusion: 函数不等式沿 Wasserstein 测地线具有可精细刻画的凸性/传递性质，且端点成立一般不能推出内点成立；结果为传输熵不等式与 Lᵖ-Poincaré 型不等式提供了统一框架与锐利常数。

Abstract: We study functional inequalities along Wasserstein geodesics. If $μ$ 0 and $μ$ 1 are respectively $κ$ 0 -and $κ$ 1 -strongly log-concave probability measures on R n , we prove that their quadratic Wasserstein geodesic satisfies  for all probability measures $ν$ on R n . The coefficient is sharp. By linearization, this recovers the Poincar{é} estimate of Han and Zhu [15]. On the real line, we prove convexity of the square roots of the optimal T 1 and T 2 constants along monotone interpolation between arbitrary probability measures. The argument applies to more general transport entropy inequalities. We also establish convexity of the rescaled L p Poincar{é} constants for every finite p $\ge$ 1, including the square root of the Poincar{é} constant and the inverse Cheeger constant. Finally, we construct a planar Wasserstein geodesic whose endpoints satisfy T 2 and all finite-p L p -Poincar{é} inequalities, whereas every interior interpolant fails these inequalities.

</details>


### [109] [A Grassmann formula for the spectral order of matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39659)
*Mohamed Amine Aouichaoui,Eun-Young Lee*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明了 $(A\join B)\oplus(A\meet B)$ 与 $A\oplus B$ 酉等价，由此导出谱序下迹与 Schatten $p$-范数的等式刻画条件及 Frobenius 内积公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Olson 谱序下矩阵 join 与 meet 的代数结构，并回答 Bourin 与 Lee 提出的关于正半定矩阵迹等式 $\Tr(A\join B)=\Tr(A+B)$ 成立条件的问题。

Method: 以 Hermitian 矩阵的 Olson 谱序为框架，证明直和恒等式 $(A\join B)\oplus(A\meet B)\cong A\oplus B$（酉等价），并通过取迹、迭代到有限矩阵族、以及考察 Schatten $p$-范数来推导各类等式成立的充要条件。

Result: 对正半定矩阵，$\Tr(A\join B)=\Tr(A+B)$ 当且仅当 $A\meet B=0$；有限正矩阵族的谱上确界迹等于和的迹当且仅当其值域构成代数直和；对固定 $1<p<\infty$，谱上确界与和的 Schatten $p$-范数相等当且仅当值域两两正交；并给出 Frobenius 内积与谱序的优美公式。

Conclusion: 直和恒等式统一刻画了谱序下 join/meet 的迹与范数性质，迹等式对应于值域的直和结构，$p$-范数等式对应于值域的正交性，深化了对 Olson 谱序与矩阵范数关系的理解。

Abstract: For Hermitian matrices $A$ and $B$, we prove that $(A\join B)\oplus(A\meet B)$ is unitarily equivalent to $A\oplus B$, where the join and meet are taken in Olson's spectral order. Taking traces answers a question of Bourin and Lee: for positive semidefinite matrices, $\Tr(A\join B)=\Tr(A+B)$ if and only if $A\meet B=0$. Iterating the direct-sum identity gives a trace formula for a finite family of positive matrices. The trace of the spectral supremum equals the trace of the sum precisely when the ranges form an algebraic direct sum. For each fixed $1<p<\infty$, the spectral supremum and the sum have equal Schatten $p$-norms if and only if the ranges are pairwise orthogonal. Finally we establish an elegant formula for the Frobenius inner product and the spectral order.

</details>


### [110] [The Families of $Γ_n$-Contractions Having Non-commutative Fundamental Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39711)
*Bhaskar Paul*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文构造了两族 2×2 矩阵 Γ_n-压缩，其基本算子既不满足交换性，也不满足给定的算子恒等式，说明这些条件只是 Γ_n-等距膨胀存在的充分而非必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Γ_n-等距膨胀理论中，通常借助基本算子的交换性及一组算子恒等式来判定膨胀的存在性。作者希望澄清这些条件的必要性问题。

Method: 定义矩阵 Γ_n-压缩为联合特征值落在 Γ_n 中的 2×2 矩阵组，并显式构造两族这样的压缩，检验其基本算子是否满足交换性与所述恒等式。

Result: 所构造的两族 2×2 矩阵 Γ_n-压缩的基本算子不满足交换条件，也不满足式 F_iF^*_{n-j} - F_jF^*_{n-i} = F^*_{n-j}F_i - F^*_{n-i}F_j（1≤i,j≤n-1），但这样的压缩确实存在。

Conclusion: 交换性与上述基本算子条件并非 Γ_n-等距膨胀存在的必要条件，仅是充分条件。

Abstract: We call an $n$-tuple of $2 \times 2$ matrices a ``\textit{$2 \times 2$ matrix $Γ_n$-contraction}'' if its joint eigenvalues (joint spectrum for a commuting tuple of matrices) are contained in $Γ_n$. In this short note on $Γ_n$-contraction, we produce two families of $2 \times 2$ matrix $Γ_n$-contractions whose fundamental operators do not satisfy the commutativity conditions and
  \begin{equation*}
  \begin{aligned}
  F_iF^*_{n-j} - F_jF^*_{n-i} = F^*_{n-j}F_i - F^*_{n-i}F_j, \quad 1 \le i, j \le n-1,
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation*}
  which shows that the commutativity as well as the conditions on fundamental operators mentioned above are sufficient, but not necessary, for the existence of $Γ_n$-isometric dilation.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [An Improved Exponent for Products of Differences over Arbitrary Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38244)
*Yao Zhi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文改进了有限域子集$A$在$|A|>q^{2/3-\delta}$条件下确保乘积集$(A-A)(A-A)$大于$q/2$的显式阈值，将$\delta$的下界从$1/13542$提升到$1/1689$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Murphy与Petridis已证明当$\delta<1/13542$且$q$充分大时，$|A|>q^{2/3-\delta}$可推出$|(A-A)(A-A)|>q/2$。本文旨在改进这一显式节约常数，使结论在更大的$\delta$范围内成立，从而加强该加法组合学结果。

Method: 证明将大能量分支中原本的物理空间抽取替换为公共Fourier谱方法，随后采用流行的比率型Balog–Szemerédi–Gowers步骤；对所得的加法能量逐层处理，保留差的重数，并结合Reiher–Schoen关系引理与Mohammadi的差–比率估计。

Result: 得到结构指数$188$，从而对绝对常数$C>0$有：$|A|\ge Cq^{375/563}=Cq^{2/3-1/1689}$蕴含$|(A-A)(A-A)|>q/2$，即显式节约从$1/13542$改进为$1/1689$。

Conclusion: 通过Fourier谱框架与逐层能量分析，本文显著提升了该问题的显式定量界，扩大了保证乘积集大小的集合规模范围。

Abstract: Murphy and Petridis proved that, for subsets $A$ of an arbitrary finite field $\F_q$, the condition $\abs A>q^{2/3-δ}$ implies $\abs{(A-A)(A-A)}>q/2$ whenever $δ<1/13542$ and $q$ is sufficiently large. We improve the explicit saving to $1/1689$. The proof replaces the main physical-space extraction in their large-energy branch by a common Fourier spectrum, followed by a popular-ratio Balog--Szemerédi--Gowers step. The resulting additive energy is treated level by level, retaining difference multiplicities and combining the Reiher--Schoen relation lemma with Mohammadi's difference--ratio estimate. This gives the structural exponent $188$ and hence \[ \abs A\ge Cq^{375/563}=Cq^{2/3-1/1689} \quad\Longrightarrow\quad \abs{(A-A)(A-A)}>\frac q2 \] for an absolute constant $C>0$.

</details>


### [112] [On the Burning Game: Nordhaus-Gaddum Bounds and Graph Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38255)
*Nina Chiarelli,Vesna Iršič Chenoweth,Marko Jakovac,William B. Kinnersley,Mirjana Mikalački*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文继续研究图的燃烧博弈，定义并研究 Burner 与 Staller 轮流选点下的博弈燃烧数，建立了其 Nordhaus-Gaddum 型界，并给出强积、笛卡尔积、词典积与冠积四类图乘积下的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在燃烧博弈已有研究基础上，进一步刻画博弈燃烧数在补图关系和图乘积结构下的变化规律，补充对其上下界与乘积图行为的理论认识。

Method: 以 Burner 与 Staller 轮流选点、火势按燃烧过程向未燃邻点传播的博弈模型为框架，采用博弈燃烧数 $b_{\rm g}(G)$ 与 Staller 先手情形的 $b_{\rm g}'(G)$，通过理论推导与组合分析建立 Nordhaus-Gaddum 界及四类图乘积（强积、笛卡尔积、词典积、冠积）的界。

Result: 建立了博弈燃烧数的 Nordhaus-Gaddum 上下界，并给出了强积、笛卡尔积、词典积和冠积四种图乘积下博弈燃烧数的界。

Conclusion: 博弈燃烧数在补图与多种图乘积运算下具有可刻画的界，扩展了燃烧博弈的理论结果。

Abstract: We continue research on the burning game on graphs. Given a graph $G$, two players, Burner and Staller, take turns in selecting vertices of $G$ to burn. All burned vertices spread fire to unburned neighboring vertices, as in the burning process. The goal of Burner is to burn the graph as quickly as possible, while Staller wants the process to last as long as possible. If both players play optimally, then the number of time steps needed to burn the whole graph $G$ is the game burning number $b_{\rm g}(G)$ if Burner makes the first move, and the Staller-start game burning number $b_{\rm g}'(G)$ if Staller starts.
  In this paper, we study this game further, establishing Nordhaus-Gaddum bounds on the game burning number, as well as bounds for four different types of graph products: strong, Cartesian, lexicographic and corona products.

</details>


### [113] [Counterexamples to Aigner's majorization conjecture for star-forest search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38284)
*Fedor Karpelevitch*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文否证了Aigner关于星森林结构下自适应定量群测试中支配条件充分性的猜想，构造了6次测试的反例及对任意k≥6的解析反例族，并证明了k≤5时该条件确实充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在恰含两个缺陷项的自适应定量群测试中，Aigner针对缺陷对构成星森林边的配置，给出了有序星大小必须满足的支配（majorization）必要条件，并猜想其充分。本文旨在检验该猜想是否成立，确定支配条件单独成立的测试预算范围。

Method: 通过构造显式的6次测试反例来否证猜想，并给出对每个测试预算k≥6都成立的解析反例族；反例利用两个紧的前缀和，对首次测试结果施加不相容的要求。此外，结合穷举计算与解析归约，证明k在0到5之间时逆命题（支配条件充分）成立。

Result: Aigner的猜想不成立：6次测试即出现反例，且对任意k≥6存在解析反例族；k≤5时支配条件充分。因此6是支配条件单独失效的第一个测试预算。反例及其无限扩展不依赖于穷举计算。

Conclusion: 支配（majorization）条件并非星森林结构下可识别缺陷对的充分条件，其充分性仅在k≤5时成立；6是首个反例出现的测试预算。该结论为自适应定量群测试的测试设计界定了支配准则的适用范围。

Abstract: In adaptive quantitative group testing with exactly two defective items, each test reports how many defectives lie in a chosen subset. We study configurations in which the possible defective pairs form the edges of a star forest. Aigner proved a necessary majorization condition on the ordered star sizes for identifying the defective pair within a prescribed number of tests, and conjectured that this condition was sufficient. We disprove the conjecture by an explicit six-test counterexample and give an analytic family of counterexamples for every test budget $k\ge6$. The obstruction uses two tight prefix sums to force incompatible demands on the outcomes of the first test. We also prove, by exhaustive computation combined with analytic reductions, that the converse holds for $0\le k\le5$. Thus six is the first test budget at which majorization alone fails. The counterexamples and their infinite extension do not depend on the exhaustive computation.

</details>


### [114] [The Burr-Erdős-Graham-Sós conjecture for the seven-cycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38286)
*Asad Shahab*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了 Burr–Erdős–Graham–Sós 猜想中剩余的 $k=3$ 情形：$f(n,\lfloor n^2/4\rfloor+1,C_7)=(1/8+o(1))n^2$，并给出了该猜想对所有固定 $k\ge3$ 的 Lean 4 形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burr、Erdős、Graham 和 Sós 猜想对每个固定 $k\ge3$，$f(n,\lfloor n^2/4\rfloor+1,C_{2k+1})=(1/8+o(1))n^2$。Bucić、Chen 和 Ma 最近证明了 $k\ge4$ 的全部情形，但 $k=3$（即 $C_7$）仍然开放，本文旨在解决这一剩余情形并推进猜想的完整形式化验证。

Method: 下界基于一个加权调色板不等式，作者通过五个采样顶点上的精确有理数证书来证明它，其主要构件是相容三角形边的分数匹配以及附着在非三角形边上的私有资源。该不等式的一个稳定形式结合正则性引理、三角形移除以及针对接近二部图的直接论证，将界迁移到任意边染色。此外，作者用 Lean 4 对 $k\ge3$ 的猜想进行了形式化，将新的七圈证明与 Bucić–Chen–Ma 关于 $k\ge4$ 论证的形式化相结合。

Result: 证明了剩余情形 $k=3$：$f(n,\lfloor n^2/4\rfloor+1,C_7)=(1/8+o(1))n^2$，从而在所有固定 $k\ge3$ 上完成了该猜想。同时给出了对应猜想的 Lean 4 形式化。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了 Burr–Erdős–Graham–Sós 猜想在 $k=3$ 时的空白，使该猜想对所有固定 $k\ge3$ 得到证明，并通过 Lean 4 形式化增强了结果的可验证性。

Abstract: For a graph $H$, let $f(n,e,H)$ be the least number of colors in an edge-coloring of some $n$-vertex graph with at least $e$ edges in which every copy of $H$ is rainbow. Burr, Erdős, Graham, and Sós conjectured that $f(n,\lfloor n^2/4\rfloor+1,C_{2k+1})=(1/8+o(1))n^2$ for every fixed $k\ge3$, and Bucić, Chen, and Ma recently proved this for all $k\ge4$. We prove the remaining case $k=3$: \[
  f\left(n,\left\lfloor n^2/4\right\rfloor+1,C_7\right)
  =\left(\frac18+o(1)\right)n^2. \] The lower bound rests on a weighted palette inequality, which we prove with an exact rational certificate on five sampled vertices. Its main ingredients are a fractional matching of compatible triangular edges and private resources attached to nontriangular edges. A stable form of the inequality, combined with regularity, triangle removal, and a direct argument for graphs close to bipartite, transfers the bound to arbitrary edge-colorings. We also describe a Lean 4 formalization of the conjecture for every fixed $k\ge3$, which combines the new seven-cycle proof with a formalization of the Bucić-Chen-Ma argument for $k\ge4$.

</details>


### [115] [Grassmannian tree webs and cluster variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38398)
*Jian-Rong Li,Chenglu Wang,Lauren Williams*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文重新证明了Gr(3,n)中平面树状web不变量的cluster/frozen变量性质，并将其推广至Gr(4,n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grassmannian齐次坐标环是簇代数的重要自然实例，但其cluster变量除特殊情形外尚不清楚。Fomin-Pylyavskyy猜想cluster与frozen变量恰为可乔木化的不可分解非椭圆web不变量，并证明了SL3平面树张量图的情形。

Method: 利用[EZLP+23]和[EZPT+25]中的cluster拟同态（upper promotion与spurion promotion），以及一个新的6-leg pattern映射，对Gr(3,n)平面树状web给出新证明并推广到Gr(4,n)。

Result: 给出了Gr(3,n)平面树状web结论的新证明；并证明：若Gr(4,n)的SL4张量图是平面树状web，则其web不变量是cluster或frozen变量。

Conclusion: 将Fomin-Pylyavskyy关于平面树状web不变量的结果从SL3推广到SL4情形，推进了对Grassmannian簇代数cluster变量的理解。

Abstract: Homogeneous coordinate rings of Grassmannians are among the most fundamental instances of cluster algebras arising in ``nature.'' Despite their importance, however, the cluster variables of these cluster algebras are not well-understood, except in special cases. For the Grassmannian of 3-planes in n-space, Fomin and Pylyavskyy conjectured [FP16] that the cluster and frozen variables are exactly the indecomposable, non-elliptic web invariants which are arborizable. They proved that in particular, if an SL3 tensor diagram is a planar tree, its web invariant is a cluster or frozen variable. In this article, we give a new proof of their result for Gr(3, n) planar tree webs, and we generalize the result to Gr(4, n): if an SL4 tensor diagram for Gr(4, n) is a planar tree web, its web invariant is a cluster or frozen variable. Our proof uses some of the cluster quasihomomorphisms from [EZLP+23] and [EZPT+25] (namely, upper promotion and spurion promotion), as well as a new 6-leg pattern map.

</details>


### [116] [The List Total Colouring Conjecture is False](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38417)
*Jonathan A. Noel*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 构造一个三阶图，其全色数为4而列表全色数为5，从而推翻了二十世纪90年代末提出的列表全染色猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列表全染色猜想（Borodin–Kostochka–Woodall、Juvan–Mohar–Škrekovski、Hilton–Johnson 于90年代后期提出）此前长期成立，本文旨在给出反例以检验该猜想的正确性。

Method: 构造一个三阶图，计算其全色数并确定其列表全色数，比较二者是否相等以寻找反例。

Result: 所构造的三阶图全色数等于4，而列表全色数为5，两者不相等。

Conclusion: 该反例推翻了列表全染色猜想，说明全色数与列表全色数在三阶图上可以不同。

Abstract: We exhibit a cubic graph with total chromatic number equal to four and list total chromatic number five. This disproves the List Total Colouring Conjecture of Borodin--Kostochka--Woodall, Juvan--Mohar--Škrekovski and Hilton--Johnson from the late 1990s. ChatGPT 6 Astra Ultra discovered the counterexample with little input from the author.

</details>


### [117] [A combinatorial interpolation between the hypercube and the associahedron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38456)
*Lara Bossinger,Ömer Gürdoğan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究一族介于超立方体与结合面体之间的多面体，给出其正规扇的显式描述，并以弦图刻画其组合结构，得到推广 Pell 数、渐近于 Catalan 数的数列；同时用星形细分描述正规扇的过渡，并将其与 Dyck 路径多面体及超图多面体相联系，证明该族多面体均给出二元几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超立方体与结合面体是组合学与多面体理论中的两个重要极端对象，二者之间存在丰富的插值结构。作者希望构造并理解一类介于二者之间的多面体族，揭示其组合性质、扇结构及其与其他已知多面体（Dyck 路径多面体、超图多面体）的联系，并探讨其与二元几何的关系。

Method: 作者构造了一族在超立方体与结合面体之间插值的多面体，显式描述其正规扇（normal fans），并利用顶点对应的弦图（chord diagrams）刻画组合结构。进一步通过星形细分（star subdivisions）描述正规扇之间的过渡，并将这些多面体与 Dyck 路径相关的多面体及超图多面体（hypergraphic polytopes）建立联系，最后论证该族多面体给出二元几何。

Result: 给出了该族多面体正规扇的显式描述及基于弦图的组合刻画；由此得到推广 Pell 数且渐近趋于 Catalan 数的数列；以星形细分刻画正规扇的过渡；建立了与 Dyck 路径多面体和超图多面体的联系；并证明该族所有多面体均给出二元几何。

Conclusion: 该工作表明超立方体与结合面体之间存在结构丰富、组合上可显式刻画的插值多面体族，其正规扇、弦图描述、数列性质及与其他多面体族的联系共同揭示了一类具有二元几何的重要多面体结构。

Abstract: We study a family of polytopes interpolating between the hypercube and the associahedron. We give an explicit description of their normal fans and describe their combinatorics in terms of chord diagrams associated to their vertices. This yields interesting number sequences generalizing Pell numbers and asymptotic to Catalan numbers. We describe the transition between the normal fans in terms of star subdivisions and relate them to polytopes associated to Dyck paths as in [Veronica Calvo Cortes and Hadleigh Frost. Dyck paths, Configuration Spaces and Polytopes for Linear Nakayama algebras. https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04571] and hypergraphic polytopes defined in [Carolina Benedetti, Nantel Bergeron, and John Machacek. Hypergraphic polytopes: combinatorial properties and antipode. Journal of Combinatorics. 2019]. Furthermore, all polytopes in our families yield binary geometries.

</details>


### [118] [Certified exact bounds for adaptive quantitative group testing with two defectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38489)
*Fedor Karpelevitch*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究至多 k 次自适应定量测试（每次报告选定子集中的缺陷数）下，能够始终识别恰好两个缺陷项的最大群体规模，完整求解了 0≤k≤10 的情形，其中 8、9、10 次测试的确切最大值分别为 65、112、192。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在组合群测试/定量测试框架下，确定有限测试预算所能支持的最大可分辨群体规模是核心理论问题；已有结果对恰好两个缺陷项且测试次数较小（k≤10）的确切边界尚不完整，作者旨在补全这一空白并给出可验证的精确刻画。

Method: 采用自适应定量测试模型，每次测试报告所选子集中的缺陷数量；通过搜索算法确定最大可识别规模，并给出显式测试策略与最大性证书，且证书由与搜索实现独立的程序进行校验；额外给出 9 次测试的部分边界以及 11 次测试对 328 项的构造性策略。

Result: 完整确定了 0≤k≤10 时恰好两个缺陷项可被始终识别的最大群体规模；8、9、10 次测试的确切最大值分别为 65、112、192；并分类出在至多 8 次测试预算内可分辨的所有“两个不相交集合各含一个缺陷项”的尺寸对。

Conclusion: 给出了恰好两个缺陷项自适应定量测试在 k≤10 下的精确容量边界，并通过独立校验的策略与最大性证书加以支持；9 次测试的边界仅部分确定，11 次测试对 328 项的构造已给出但其最大性仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We determine the maximum population size for which exactly two defective items can always be identified using at most $k$ adaptive quantitative tests, for every $0\le k\le10$. Each test reports the number of defectives in a chosen subset. The exact maxima for eight, nine and ten tests are 65, 112 and 192, respectively. We also classify all pairs of sizes of two disjoint sets, each containing exactly one defective, that can be resolved within each test budget through eight. These exact results are supported by explicit strategies and maximality certificates checked by programs separate from the search implementation. Additional results include partial nine-test boundaries and an eleven-test strategy for 328 items; maximality of the latter remains open.

</details>


### [119] [One and Seven-Eighths Divisibility Problems of Propp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38493)
*Aidan Dunkelberg*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文完整解决Jim Propp 1999年文章中的问题30，并近乎完整解决问题31，均涉及两类非二部三角图匹配数的整除性质；进一步推广证明：由四面体单元组成、且单元数相对顶点数足够少的图，其匹配数可被3的幂整除，并据此构造了一类匹配数被3整除的非平面图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jim Propp在1999年综述"Enumeration of matchings; problems and progress"中提出了关于非二部三角图匹配数整除性质的开放问题（问题30、31），这些问题尚无完整解答，作者希望推进该方向并揭示匹配数整除性与图结构的联系。

Method: 作者针对问题30给出完整证明，对问题31给出近乎完整的证明；随后将问题31的证明方法推广，用于分析由四面体单元构成的图，利用"单元数相对于顶点数足够少"的条件，推导匹配数被3的幂整除的结论。

Result: 完整解决问题30，几乎完整解决问题31；并证明任意由四面体单元组成、且单元数相对于顶点数足够少的图，其匹配数被3的幂整除；由此得到一族匹配数被3整除的非平面图。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了Propp提出的匹配计数整除性问题，将方法推广至更广的四面体单元图类，并首次给出匹配数被3整除的非平面图例子，扩展了匹配数整除性质的研究范围。

Abstract: We provide one full solution, and another nearly complete solution, to two problems from Jim Propp's 1999 article "Enumeration of matchings; problems and progress" (namely, Problems 30 and 31) involving divisibility properties of the number of matchings of two non-bipartite triangular graphs. We extend our method of proof of the second problem to show that the number of matchings of any graph composed of tetrahedral cells, such that the number of cells is suitably few relative to the number of vertices, is divisible by a power of 3; in particular, we then exhibit a collection of non-planar graphs whose number of matchings is divisible by 3.

</details>


### [120] [Does the Endomorphism Ordered Set of a Finite Ordered Set Determine the Ordered Set? The Cases of Height $1$ and "Trebled" Ordered Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38532)
*Jonathan David Farley*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究有限偏序集函数空间的同构问题，证明在特定条件下 $P^P\cong Q^Q$ 蕴含 $P\cong Q$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有限序集的函数空间（保序映射构成的序集）同构是否能唯一确定原序集，即研究 $P^P\cong Q^Q \Rightarrow P\cong Q$ 在何种条件下成立。

Method: 对有序集 $X,Y$，定义 $Y^X$ 为保序映射构成的序集（逐点序）。设 $P,Q$ 为有限有序集且 $P^P\cong Q^Q$，分情形证明 $P\cong Q$。

Result: 证明：若 $P$ 或 $Q$ 的高度至多为 $1$，或 $P,Q$ 是将某一有序集的每个元素替换为三元反链所得到的序集，则 $P^P\cong Q^Q$ 蕴含 $P\cong Q$。

Conclusion: 在所述条件下函数空间同构可推出原序集同构；其中后一情形是对 Tim Campion 证明的细化与推广。

Abstract: For ordered sets $X$ and $Y$, let $Y^X$ denote the ordered set of order-preserving maps from $X$ to $Y$, where $f\le g$ in $Y^X$ if $f(x)\le g(x)$ for all $x\in X$.
  Let $P$ and $Q$ be finite ordered sets such that $P^P\cong Q^Q$. It is proven that $P\cong Q$ if $P$ or $Q$ has height at most $1$ or if $P$ and $Q$ are ordered sets of the following form: replace each element of an ordered set with a three-element antichain. The latter is an elaboration of a proof of Tim Campion.

</details>


### [121] [On c-polynomial factorisations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38567)
*Harry G. Hylock,Matthew C. Lettington,Karl Michael Schmidt*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将c-多项式(x^n-1)/(x-1)分解为c-不可约因子的结构定理，与整数n的联合有序分解相联系，并给出路径图色多项式的精确表达及素数、无平方因子约束下的计数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以Carlitz与Moser的定理为动机，研究c-多项式的因子分解结构，并探讨其与整数n的联合有序分解之间的关联及计数问题。

Method: 将c-多项式的c-分解与由整数n产生的联合有序分解相联系，证明结构定理；通过计算不同联合有序分解的数目，导出路径图的精确色多项式（在m种颜色且全部使用的条件下计数路径图着色方式）。

Result: 得到(x^n-1)/(x-1)分解为c-不可约因子的结构定理；给出路径图的精确色多项式；并给出在所有因子均为素数或均为无平方因子的约束下联合有序分解的类似计数公式。

Conclusion: c-多项式分解结构与整数n的联合有序分解存在深刻联系，相关计数问题可用路径图色多项式精确刻画，并可推广至素数、无平方因子等约束情形。

Abstract: c-Polynomials are polynomials whose (non-zero) coefficients are all equal to 1. Taking a theorem by Carlitz and Moser as a motivation, we prove a structure theorem for the factorisations of the c-polynomial $(x^n-1)/(x-1)$ into c-irreducible factors by relating c-torisations into c-polynomials to joint ordered factorisations arising from the integer n. The question of how many different joint ordered factorisations there are leads to the precise chromatic polynomial for path graphs, counting the ways of path graph colourings with $m$ colours under the condition that all colours are used. Moreover, we give similar counting formulae for joint ordered factorisations under the constraints that all factors are primes, or that all factors are square-free.

</details>


### [122] [A rainbow partition theorem for trees and connected maximin share allocations of chores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38628)
*Marcin Anholcer,Maciej Bartkowiak,Bartłomiej Bosek,Jarosław Grytczuk,Zbigniew Lonc,Paweł Rzążewski*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文正面回答了树上的不可分家务在连通捆绑下能否实现连通最大最小份额的公开问题，并证明了一个更强的组合定理，其证明基于有向树的内向划分与彩色KKM定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Xiao, Qiu 与 Huang（AAMAS 2023）以及 Lonc 独立提出了一个公开问题：位于树顶点上的不可分家务，能否总被分配给 n 个代理，使得每个代理获得连通捆绑，且其成本不超过其连通最大最小份额（connected maximin share）。对于物品（goods），这已是 Bouveret 等人的定理；对于家务（chores）则尚未解决。本文旨在回答该问题。

Method: 作者将问题归约到一个组合定理：若 P_1,…,P_k 是有限树顶点集的划分，每个划分至多含 k 个连通部分，则顶点集可拆分为互不相交的集合 B_1,…,B_k（可空），使每个非空 B_i 连通且包含于 P_i 的某部分中。等价地，若 k 种颜色中每种至多出现在树的 k−1 条边上，则顶点可划分为以不同颜色标记的连通集合，且每个集合不含自身颜色的边。证明将问题归约为有向树的内向划分（inward partitions），后者通过在边权单纯形上使用彩色 KKM 定理（colourful KKM theorem）并结合叶消除标记法得到，该标记在权重消失时仍保持兼容。作者还给出了运行时间为 k^{O(k)} 加多项式时间的算法。

Result: 作者肯定地回答了上述公开问题，且结论对单调成本（monotone costs）也成立。组合定理的界在路径（paths）上已是最优；对于可加成本，该定理与公平分配命题等价。

Conclusion: 树上的不可分家务在连通捆绑下总能实现连通最大最小份额，即使成本仅为单调的；该公平分配结果由一个更一般的组合定理推出，后者刻画了树上顶点集按颜色连通划分的存在性条件，并通过彩色 KKM 定理与内向划分技术证明。

Abstract: Xiao, Qiu, and Huang (AAMAS 2023) and independently Lonc (personal communication) asked whether indivisible chores located at the vertices of a tree can always be allocated to $n$ agents in connected bundles so that the cost of every agent is at most its connected maximin share; for goods, this is a theorem of Bouveret, Cechlárová, Elkind, Igarashi, and Peters. We answer the question affirmatively, even for monotone costs. The answer follows from a combinatorial theorem: if $\mathcal P_1,\ldots,\mathcal P_k$ are partitions of the vertex set of a finite tree, each into at most $k$ connected parts, then the vertex set can be split into disjoint sets $B_1,\ldots,B_k$, some possibly empty, such that each nonempty $B_i$ is connected and contained in a part of $\mathcal P_i$. Equivalently, if each of $k$ colours occurs on at most $k-1$ edges of a tree, then the vertices can be partitioned into connected sets labelled by distinct colours, none containing an edge of its own colour; in particular, one can choose for every colour a component of the forest obtained by deleting that colour so that the chosen components cover the tree. The bound is already best possible for paths, and for additive costs, the theorem is equivalent to the fair-division statement. The proof reduces the problem to inward partitions of oriented trees, which we obtain from the colourful KKM theorem on a simplex of edge weights, using a leaf-elimination labelling that remains compatible when weights vanish. We also give an algorithm running in time $k^{O(k)}$ plus polynomial time.

</details>


### [123] [Counting hypergraphs without linear cycles of fixed length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38772)
*József Balogh,Ramon I. Garcia,Abhishek Methuku*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文完全解决了Balogh-Narayanan-Skokan提出的问题：对任意整数$r,k\ge 3$，$n$个标号顶点上的$C_k^{(r)}$-free $r$-图数量为$2^{(1+o(1))\operatorname{ex}_{r}(n,C_{k}^{(r)})}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Balogh、Narayanan和Skokan提出问题：对任意$r,k\ge 3$，$C_k^{(r)}$-free $r$-图的数量是否等于$2^{(1+o(1))\operatorname{ex}_{r}(n,C_{k}^{(r)})}$。图的类似陈述（$r=2$）已被Morris和Saxton的构造所否定，而超图的一般情形长期悬而未决，最近Jiang和Longbrake仅在$r\geq5$时给出肯定答案。

Method: 当$(r,k)\neq(3,3)$时，建立线性圈的balanced supersaturation结果，并结合超图container方法。当$(r,k)=(3,3)$时，按pair-codegrees分解$3$-图，将包含大codegree对的超边所构成的子超图编码为有向图，并应用multicolour entropy定理。

Result: 证明了该问题对每一对整数$r,k\ge 3$的答案均为肯定，从而完全解决了该问题。

Conclusion: 论文彻底回答了Balogh-Narayanan-Skokan的问题，表明尽管图（$r=2$）情形下类似结论不成立，但对于所有$r,k\ge3$的超图情形，$C_k^{(r)}$-free $r$-图数量确实为$2^{(1+o(1))\operatorname{ex}_{r}(n,C_{k}^{(r)})}$。

Abstract: Let $C_{k}^{(r)}$ be the $r$-uniform linear cycle on $k$ hyperedges. An $r$-graph is $C_{k}^{(r)}$-free if it contains no copy of $C_{k}^{(r)}$. Let $\operatorname{ex}_{r}(n,C_{k}^{(r)})$ denote the maximum number of hyperedges in an $n$-vertex $C_{k}^{(r)}$-free $r$-graph. Balogh, Narayanan and Skokan asked whether, for every pair of integers $r,k\ge 3$, the number of $C_k^{(r)}$-free $r$-graphs on $n$ labelled vertices is \[ 2^{(1+o(1))\operatorname{ex}_{r}(n,C_{k}^{(r)})}. \] While the analogous statement is known to fail for graphs ($r=2$), by a construction of Morris and Saxton, the general question remained open for hypergraphs. Very recently, Jiang and Longbrake answered the question affirmatively when $r\geq5$.
  In this paper, we completely resolve the problem by proving that the answer is affirmative for every pair of integers $r,k\geq3$. When $(r,k)\neq(3,3)$, we establish balanced supersaturation results for linear cycles and combine them with the hypergraph container method. For $(r,k)=(3,3)$, we instead decompose $3$-graphs according to their pair-codegrees, encode the subhypergraph formed by the hyperedges that contain a large codegree pair as a directed graph, and apply a multicolour entropy theorem.

</details>


### [124] [Upper bounds for ordered Ramsey numbers of forests and bounded-degree graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38828)
*Lior Gishboliner,Xiangyu Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了两个有序Ramsey数（ordered Ramsey numbers）的上界：对有序森林和有界最大度、有界区间色数的固定有序图给出多项式上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有序Ramsey数是经典Ramsey数的推广，但相关上界结果相对稀少。此前Geneson等人提出关于有序森林的Ramsey数上下界问题，本文旨在解决该问题并进一步推广到更一般的图类。

Method: 利用有序图的结构参数（区间色数、最大度）进行分析，通过组合论证给出有序Ramsey数的上界估计。

Result: (1) 任意n个顶点的有序森林F满足 R_<(F,F)=O(n^{1+⌈log χ_<(F)⌉})，从而回答了Geneson等人的问题；(2) 存在函数f，使得对每个固定有序图H（最大度≤Δ、区间色数≤k）有 R_<(H,K_n)=O_H(n^{f(Δ,k)})。

Conclusion: 本文给出了有序森林及有界最大度与有界区间色数的有序图的有序Ramsey数的多项式上界，推进了有序Ramsey数的研究。

Abstract: We prove the following two upper bounds for ordered Ramsey numbers:
  (1) Every ordered forest $F$ on $n$ vertices satisfies $R_{<}(F,F)=O(n^{1+\lceil\logχ_{<}(F)\rceil})$. This in particular answers a question of Geneson, Holmes, Liu, Neidinger, Pehova and Wass.
  (2) There is a function $f$ such that, for every fixed ordered graph $H$ with maximum degree at most $Δ$ and interval chromatic number at most $k$, it holds that $R_{<}(H,K_n)=O_H(n^{f(Δ,k)})$.

</details>


### [125] [New interpretations for Kromatic symmetric function expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38876)
*Aarush Kulkarni,Laura Pierson*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究Kromatic对称函数（KSF）在两种基下的展开公式，并给出图Hopf代数的K-类比antipode公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KSF是Crew、Pechenik和Spirkl（2026）引入的染色对称函数（CSF）的K-理论类比。已有工作（第二作者与Samanta）用KSF在K-理论单项对称函数基下的递推公式证明KSF能区分CSF无法区分的图族，但相关展开公式和组合解释尚需系统推广与完善。

Method: 首先探索递推方法计算KSF在K-理论单项对称函数基$\overline{\widetilde{m}}_\lambda$下的展开，推广既有公式；其次采用类似Stanley（1995）的容斥方法（而非无环定向方法）为KSF在K-理论幂和基$\overline{p}_\lambda$下的展开给出新解释；最后给出Schmitt以及Humpert-Martin关于图Hopf代数antipode公式的K-类比，并为两种展开提供Hopf代数解释。

Result: 建立了KSF在$\overline{\widetilde{m}}_\lambda$基下的递推展开公式，给出了$\overline{p}_\lambda$基展开的容斥新解释，并得到了图Hopf代数antipode公式的K-类比及两种展开的Hopf代数解释。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了KSF在两类基下的展开，扩展了KSF区分图的能力，并通过Hopf代数框架统一了组合展开与代数结构。

Abstract: The Kromatic symmetric function (KSF) $\overline{X}_{G}$, introduced by Crew, Pechenik, and Spirkl (2026), is a $K$-theoretic analogue of the chromatic symmetric function (CSF) $X_G$. We study expansion formulas for the KSF in two different bases. First, we explore recursive ways to compute the KSF's expansion in the $K$-theoretic monomial symmetric function basis $\overline{\widetilde{m}}_λ$, generalizing formulas that were used by the second author and Samanta to show that the KSF distinguishes certain families of graphs that are not distinguished by the CSF. Second, we give new interpretations for the KSF's expansion in the $K$-theoretic power sum basis $\overline{p}_λ$, using an inclusion-exclusion approach like the one from Stanley (1995) instead of an acyclic orientation approach. Finally, we give $K$-analogues of Schmitt's and of Humpert and Martin's antipode formulas for the Hopf algebra of graphs, along with a Hopf algebra interpretation for both the $\overline{p}$-expansion and $\overline{\widetilde{m}}$-expansion of the KSF.

</details>


### [126] [Pair-Defensive Silver Colourings of Hypercubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38993)
*Saman Farhat,Mehrak Shirkhani*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究同时测试下更强的银着色要求，定义"成对防御银着色"，证明超立方体 Q_d 中该类着色最大颜色数不超过 ⌊(d+3)/2⌋，约为普通银着色目标 d+1 的一半，并在每个 2 的幂附近的四个连续维度上构造达到该界的着色，刻画奇临界维度下极值着色的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通银着色条件在逐个测试顶点时足够，但在同时测试（一个或两个被攻击顶点）情形下不足。作者希望刻画更强的局部要求：当独立集中一个或两个顶点同时被攻击时，每种颜色都必须为它们提供互不相同的邻近防御者，从而保证同时防御的有效性。以超立方体 Q_d 及一个奇偶类作为被攻击集为研究对象。

Method: 定义成对防御银着色：对独立集中任意一个或两个被攻击顶点，每种颜色须在其闭邻域内提供彼此不同的防御者。针对超立方体 Q_d，取一个奇偶类为被攻击集，采用组合与图论方法分析最大颜色数的上界，并通过构造性方法给出达到上界的着色；进而分析极值着色的结构，在奇临界维度下借助将防御者奇偶类划分为 halved cube 的正则、无三角形子图来刻画，并给出基于坐标匹配和缺陷坐标的局部形式。

Result: 证明超立方体 Q_d 中成对防御银着色的最大颜色数上界为 ⌊(d+3)/2⌋，约为普通银着色目标 d+1 的一半。在每个 2 的幂附近的四个连续维度上构造出达到该上界的着色。在奇临界维度下，给出极值着色的结构刻画（将防御者奇偶类划分为 halved cube 的正则、无三角形子图）及其基于坐标匹配与缺陷坐标的局部形式。

Conclusion: 成对防御银着色显著降低超立方体上可用的最大颜色数（约减半），说明同时测试要求比逐个测试更为严格。所构造的着色在 2 的幂附近多个维度上达到上界，且奇临界维度极值着色具有可刻画的结构，建立了与 halved cube 子图划分及坐标匹配/缺陷坐标的联系。

Abstract: A silver colouring is a proper colouring in which every colour appears in the closed neighbourhood of each vertex of a prescribed independent set. This local condition suffices when vertices are tested one at a time. We study a stronger requirement for simultaneous testing: whenever one or two vertices of the independent set are attacked together, each colour must supply distinct nearby defenders for them. We call this a pair-defensive silver colouring. For the hypercube $Q_d$, with one parity class as the attacked set, we prove that the maximum number of colours in a pair-defensive silver colouring is at most $\lfloor(d+3)/2\rfloor$, roughly half the ordinary silver-colouring target $d+1$. We construct colourings attaining this bound in four consecutive dimensions around every power of two, and we study the structure of the extremal, bound-attaining colourings. In each odd critical dimension, we characterize the extremal colourings by a partition of the defender parity into regular, triangle-free subgraphs of the halved cube. This characterization also has a local form in terms of coordinate matchings and a defect coordinate

</details>


### [127] [A double-logarithmic upper bound on the chromatic number of the associahedron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39015)
*Sang-il Oum,David R. Wood*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明结合多胞体 $\mathcal{A}_n$ 的色数为 $O(\log\log n)$，改进了此前 $O(\log n)$ 的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合多胞体是组合数学中的重要对象，其色数刻画了其1-骨架图的染色性质。此前已知上界为 $O(\log n)$，缩小该上界有助于理解结合多胞体图结构的复杂度与着色行为。

Method: 摘要未给出具体方法细节，仅陈述通过对 $\mathcal{A}_n$ 的1-骨架图进行染色分析，得到色数的渐进上界估计。

Result: $\mathcal{A}_n$ 的色数为 $O(\log\log n)$，显著优于先前的 $O(\log n)$ 上界。

Conclusion: 结合多胞体的色数上界由 $O(\log n)$ 改进至 $O(\log\log n)$，加深了对该类多胞体图染色复杂度的认识。

Abstract: We show that the associahedron $\mathcal{A}_n$ has chromatic number $O(\log\log n)$, improving on the previously best known upper bound of $O(\log n)$.

</details>


### [128] [An elementary proof of the 2-regularity of Pythagorean triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39063)
*William J. Wesley*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出了"任意正整数2-染色均存在单色勾股三元组"这一结论的新证明，采用初等技术，无需冗长计算或复杂分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该结论最初由Heule、Kullmann和Marek于2016年借助SAT求解器证明，回答了Graham数十年前提出的问题，但原证明依赖大规模计算。作者希望提供一个更简洁、可人工验证的证明。

Method: 使用初等数学技巧进行证明，不依赖冗长的计算机计算，也不依赖困难的分析机制。

Result: 成功给出了该定理的一个新的初等证明。

Conclusion: 该结论可仅用初等技术证明，表明此前必须依赖SAT求解器的结果存在更简洁、更易检验的证明途径。

Abstract: We give a new proof of the fact that every 2-coloring of the positive integers contains a monochromatic Pythagorean triple. This result was originally proven by Heule, Kullmann, and Marek with SAT solvers in 2016, answering a decades-old question of Graham. This proof uses elementary techniques and requires neither lengthy computation nor difficult analytic machinery.

</details>


### [129] [On Eternal Connected Vertex Cover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39103)
*Rajat Adak,Saraswati Girish Nanoti*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究永恒连通顶点覆盖数$ecvc(G)$与最小连通顶点覆盖数$cvc(G)$的关系，给出了$cvc(G)$的紧界及等式成立图类的刻画，并应用得到全细分图与极小2连通图的$ecvc(G)=cvc(G)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知$cvc(G)\leq ecvc(G)\leq cvc(G)+1$，且"每个顶点属于某个最小连通顶点覆盖"是$ecvc(G)=cvc(G)$的必要条件，但其是否为充分条件尚不清楚。本文旨在判定该条件的充分性，并刻画$ecvc(G)=cvc(G)$成立的图类。

Method: 构造反例图：给出一个32顶点图$G$，其中$cvc(G)=19$、$ecvc(G)=20$，但每个顶点都属于某个最小连通顶点覆盖，说明该必要条件不充分。对最小度至少为2的连通图，证明紧界$cvc(G)\geq 2|V(G)|-|E(G)|-1$，定义取等号图类$\mathcal F$，证明$G\in\mathcal F$当且仅当度至少为3的顶点导出森林；并在$\mathcal F$内建立$ecvc(G)=cvc(G)$、每个顶点属于某最小连通顶点覆盖、每个圈上至少有两个二度顶点三者的等价性。

Result: 必要条件不充分：存在32顶点反例$G$，$cvc(G)=19$，$ecvc(G)=20$，而每个顶点均属于某最小连通顶点覆盖。对最小度≥2的连通图得到紧界$cvc(G)\geq 2|V(G)|-|E(G)|-1$。$G\in\mathcal F$当且仅当度≥3顶点导出森林。在$\mathcal F$中，$ecvc(G)=cvc(G)$、每个顶点属于某最小连通顶点覆盖、每个圈上至少有两个二度顶点三条件等价。应用得到：最小度≥2连通图的全细分图以及极小2连通图均满足$ecvc(G)=cvc(G)$，且这两类图中每个最小连通顶点覆盖都是永恒获胜配置；对$\mathcal F$外的图给出反例说明该性质一般不成立。

Conclusion: "每个顶点属于某最小连通顶点覆盖"并非$ecvc(G)=cvc(G)$的充分条件。通过紧界$cvc(G)\geq 2|V(G)|-|E(G)|-1$定义并刻画了图类$\mathcal F$，在其中给出三个等价条件，并将$ecvc(G)=cvc(G)$及"每个最小连通顶点覆盖均为永恒获胜配置"的结果推广到全细分图与极小2连通图。

Abstract: For a connected graph $G$ with at least one edge, the \textit{eternal connected vertex cover} number $ecvc(G)$ is the minimum number of guards that can maintain a connected vertex cover after every response to an arbitrary sequence of edge attacks. Denote the minimum size of a connected vertex cover by $cvc(G)$. It is known that $cvc(G)\leq ecvc(G)\leq cvc(G)+1$. A necessary condition for $ecvc(G)=cvc(G)$ is that every vertex belongs to some minimum connected vertex cover. We show that this condition is not sufficient: there exists a $32$-vertex graph $G$ that has $cvc(G)=19$ and $ecvc(G)=20$, although every vertex of $G$ belongs to some minimum connected vertex cover.
  For connected graphs with minimum degree at least two, we establish the sharp bound $cvc(G)\geq 2|V(G)|-|E(G)|-1$ and $\mathcal F$ denotes the class attaining equality. We prove that $G\in\mathcal F$ if and only if the vertices of degree at least three induce a forest. Within $\mathcal F$, the conditions $ecvc(G)=cvc(G)$, membership of every vertex in some minimum connected vertex cover, and the presence of at least two degree-two vertices on every cycle are equivalent. As applications, we obtain $ecvc(G)=cvc(G)$ for full subdivisions of connected graphs of minimum degree at least two and for minimally $2$-connected graphs. In both the families, every minimum connected vertex cover is an eternally winning configuration. This is not true in general for graphs outside $\mathcal{F}$, we show one example of such a graph.

</details>


### [130] [Diameter and radius of an additive-multiplicative graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39347)
*Yi Hu,Nan Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究正整数图的直径与半径，证明其满足 6≤diam(G)≤7 与 4≤rad(G)≤5；在 Dickson 猜想下精确确定为 diam(G)=6 和 rad(G)=4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注一个由正整数自然构造的数论图（差值或商为素数），希望刻画其距离性质。此类问题能建立素数分布与图的不变量之间的联系，从而定量理解图的结构。

Method: 定义图 G：正整数为顶点，两顶点相邻当且仅当差为 1，或较大者除以较小者为素数。通过对路径构造与素数存在性估计给出距离上下界；并引入 Dickson 关于两个线性形式的猜想，以获得精确值。

Result: 无条件结果：6≤diam(G)≤7，4≤rad(G)≤5。在 Dickson 猜想（两个线性形式）下：diam(G)=6 且 rad(G)=4。

Conclusion: 该数论图的直径与半径被精确定位在两个整数值之间；条件性结果进一步给出精确值（6 和 4），说明其距离性质主要由素数存在性假设决定。

Abstract: Let $G$ be the graph on the positive integers in which distinct vertices are adjacent if they differ by one or their quotient in one order is prime. We prove that $6\leq\operatorname{diam}(G)\leq7$ and $4\leq\operatorname{rad}(G)\leq5$. Under Dickson's conjecture for two linear forms, we prove that $\operatorname{diam}(G)=6$ and $\operatorname{rad}(G)=4$.

</details>


### [131] [Nice Partitions, Supersolvability, and Freeness in Deformations of Graphic Arrangements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39407)
*Weikang Liang,Suijie Wang,Yue Zhou*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究图构型的仿射变形及其锥，证明块状可容许性与锥的超可解性、好划分三者等价，并给出模链构造与弦图判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画图构型仿射变形的组合性质，建立可容许序、超可解性与好划分之间的等价关系，深化对超平面构型可分解性的理解。

Method: 引入块状可容许（blockwise admissible）概念，基于完美消去序；采用构型论直接证明方法，构造无穷远超平面处的极大模链。

Result: 对任意图G证明等价性：(i)块状可容许；(ii)锥cA(G_S)超可解；(iii)存在好划分。块上的好划分由可容许序诱导，其边类为具不同中心之星。若锥自由，则G为弦图。

Conclusion: 建立了仿射图构型的可容许性、超可解性与好划分的等价刻画，给出模链构造，并在自由性下得到图的弦性结论。

Abstract: We study affine deformations $\mathcal{A}(G_{\mathcal{S}})$ of graphic arrangements and their cones. Here $G=([n],E(G))$ is a simple graph, $\mathcal{S}=(S_{ij})$ is a family of finite gain sets, and $\mathcal{A}(G_{\mathcal{S}})$ consists of the hyperplanes $x_i-x_j=a$ with $a\in S_{ij}$. We call $G_{\mathcal{S}}$ blockwise admissible if every block has a vertex ordering $v_1,\ldots,v_m$ satisfying $S_{v_kv_j}-S_{v_kv_i}\subseteq S_{v_iv_j}$ for every $k$ and all distinct $i,j>k$. Every such ordering is a perfect elimination ordering. We prove, for arbitrary $G$, that the following are equivalent: (i) $G_{\mathcal{S}}$ is blockwise admissible; (ii) the cone $c\mathcal{A}(G_{\mathcal{S}})$ is supersolvable; and (iii) $\mathcal{A}(G_{\mathcal{S}})$ admits a nice partition. We give a direct arrangement-theoretic proof of these equivalences. For a block, the equivalence between admissibility and supersolvability is already contained in Zaslavsky's characterization of supersolvable graphic-lift lattices. We further show that every nice partition on a block is induced by an admissible ordering and that its induced edge classes are stars with distinct centers. Under these equivalent conditions, we construct a maximal modular chain through the hyperplane at infinity. We also prove that if $c\mathcal{A}(G_{\mathcal{S}})$ is free, then $G$ is chordal.

</details>


### [132] [Hamilton-connected cores and five cycle--wheel Ramsey numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39508)
*Zehui Shao,Hanxin Jiang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文用结构性（组合）证明确定了若干轮图与圈图的拉姆齐数：R(C14,W11)=27、R(C15,W11)=29，并结合已有定理得到 n≥14 时 R(Cn,W11)=2n−1；同时证明 R(C8,W7)=15、R(C9,W7)=17、R(C8,W9)=15，并给出 (C8,C6)-与 (C9,C6)-临界着色的完整结构分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定轮图与圈的拉姆齐数 R(Cn,Wm) 的精确值，特别是 W11 的边界情形（n=14,15），以补全 n≥16 已有定理所留下的空缺，从而给出统一公式，并避免依赖穷举式图枚举。

Method: 以 Hamilton-连通核心引理为共同起点。对十一顶点轮图 W11，利用顶点连通度界与局部圈相关二部图匹配数的界，得到阶为 9 的顶点割；再用指定端点的路径排除剩余两个顶点集的阶数组合。对较小轮图，使用临界圈着色结构与局部圈缩短论证；并完成 (C8,C6)-、(C9,C6)-临界着色的结构分类。

Result: 证明 R(C14,W11)=27 与 R(C15,W11)=29；结合 n≥16 的定理得 R(Cn,W11)=2n−1（n≥14）。另证明 R(C8,W7)=15、R(C9,W7)=17、R(C8,W9)=15；并给出 (C8,C6)-与 (C9,C6)-临界着色的完整结构分类，重现此前报道的计数 24 与 26。

Conclusion: 所给结果统一并补全了轮图-圈图拉姆齐数 R(Cn,W11) 的取值，且所有证明均为组合性的、不使用穷举图枚举，验证了结构方法在拉姆齐数精确计算中的有效性。

Abstract: Let $W_s=K_1+C_{s-1}$ denote the wheel on $s$ vertices. We give structural proofs that $R(C_{14},W_{11})=27$ and $R(C_{15},W_{11})=29$. Together with the theorem of Chen et al. for $n\ge16$, these equalities give $R(C_n,W_{11})=2n-1$ for every $n\ge14$. The two boundary values were included in an earlier survey announcement. We also give structural proofs of $R(C_8,W_7)=15$, $R(C_9,W_7)=17$, and $R(C_8,W_9)=15$. The common starting point is a Hamilton-connected core lemma. For the eleven-vertex wheel, bounds on vertex connectivity and on the matching number of a bipartite graph associated with a local cycle yield a vertex cut of order nine. Paths with prescribed endpoints then rule out every possible pair of orders of the two remaining vertex sets. For the smaller wheels, we use the structure of critical cycle colorings and local cycle-shortening arguments. We also give complete structural classifications of the $(C_8,C_6)$- and $(C_9,C_6)$-critical colorings, recovering the previously reported counts 24 and 26. All proofs are combinatorial and use no exhaustive graph enumeration.

</details>


### [133] [On the chromatic number of pseudohemisphere hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39538)
*Balázs István Szabó*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文引入伪半球超图（pseudohemisphere hypergraph）概念，证明其色数不超过4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半球超图（hemisphere hypergraph）是超图着色理论的经典研究对象，将已知结果推广到更一般的情形有助于刻画哪些超图类具有有界的色数。伪半球超图是通过对ABA-free超图及其补做有符号对称差操作得到的新类，其中ABA-free超图的结构特性使其具有特殊地位。

Method: 定义：设超图H的顶点集V带序，若存在V上的ABA-free超图F及子集X⊆V，使得H的超边集包含于{F Δ X : F ∈ F ∪ F̄}（F̄为F的补超图），则称H为伪半球超图。核心证明思路是结合ABA-free超图的结构性质与对称差变换下色数影响的控制，将伪半球超图的着色问题归约到已知的ABA-free超图着色结果上。

Result: 证明伪半球超图的色数至多为4。

Conclusion: 该文将半球超灰色数上界的经典结论推广到伪半球超图这一更广的类，给出色数≤4的统一界。

Abstract: A pseudohemisphere hypergraph is a hypergraph $\mathcal{H}$ with an ordered set of vertices $V$ for which there exists an $ABA$-free hypergraph $\mathcal{F}$ on $V$ and a subset $X$ of $V$ such that the hyperedge set of $\mathcal{H}$ is a subset of $\{FΔX: F\in \mathcal{F}\cup \overline{\mathcal{F}}\}$. We prove that the chromatic number of pseudohemisphere hypergraphs is at most four.

</details>


### [134] [Sub-quorum colorings of some infinite families of caterpillars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39619)
*Rafik Sahbi,Youcef Belkina,Amar Bennadji*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入了子法定人数着色（sub-quorum coloring）及其对应的子法定人数着色数 ψ_sq(G)，并确定了若干无限族毛毛虫图（包括完全 n 元毛毛虫图以及最小脊点度为 3 的完全毛毛虫图）的 ψ_sq(G) 精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法定人数着色（quorum coloring）要求每个顶点在闭邻域中至少有一半顶点与其同色，其最大值 ψ_q(G) 已有研究。但该定义要求全顶点分配颜色，限制较强。作者希望通过引入"部分定义"的着色概念（即子法定人数着色）来放宽约束，从而刻画更灵活的色彩分配结构，并针对毛毛虫图这类经典树的子族求出精确结果。

Method: 作者定义了子法定人数着色为到 {1,...,ℓ} 的满射部分函数 f，要求对每个顶点 v，若 f(v) 有定义，则在 N[v] 中有像的顶点里至少一半与 v 同色；ψ_sq(G) 为该类着色中 ℓ 的最大值。随后针对完全 n 元毛毛虫图（complete n-tuple caterpillars）和最小脊点度为 3 的完全毛毛虫图等无限族，通过构造性着色与上界论证相结合的方式，逐族确定 ψ_sq(G) 的精确值。

Result: 对完全 n 元毛毛虫图与最小脊点度为 3 的完全毛毛虫图等无限族毛毛虫图，给出了子法定人数着色数 ψ_sq(G) 的精确公式/精确取值。

Conclusion: 子法定人数着色数是对法定人数着色数的自然推广，通过部分着色放宽约束后可在特定图族上获得精确解。本文的结果为毛毛虫图的这一新参数提供了完整刻画，也为进一步研究其他图类的 ψ_sq(G) 提供了基础。

Abstract: A partition $π=\{V_{1},V_{2},...,V_{k}\}$ of the vertex set $V$ of a graph $G$ into $k$ color classes $V_{i},$ with $i\in\{1,...,k\}$ is called a {\it quorum coloring} if for every vertex $v\in V,$ at least half of the vertices in the closed neighborhood $N[v]$ of $v$ have the same color as $v.$ The maximum cardinality of a quorum coloring of $G$ is called the {\it quorum coloring number} of $G$ and is denoted by $ψ_{q}(G).$ A {\it sub-quorum coloring} of $G$ is an onto partial function $f:V\rightarrow\left\{1,2,\ldots,\ell\right\}$ having the property that for every vertex $v\in V,$ if $f(v)$ is defined, then at least half of the vertices in $N[v]$ having an image by $f$, have the same color as $v.$ The {\it sub-quorum coloring number} $ψ_{sq}(G)$ equals the maximum value $\ell$ in a sub-quorum coloring of $G.$ In this paper, we determine the exact value of the sub-quorum coloring number for some infinite families of caterpillars including complete $n$-tuple caterpillars and complete caterpillars with minimum spine-vertex degree three.

</details>


### [135] [A Fixed-Offset Transition for Random Stackability on Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39633)
*John Fairfax-Ball*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究路径图上的支撑坍塌型图卵石配置问题，证明堆叠概率关于对数密度存在一个以√(log₂n)−½log₂log₂n+log₂(3e)为中心的定偏移双侧相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究支撑坍塌版本的图卵石问题，旨在刻画均匀固定总量配置下把全部卵石合法移动汇聚到单一顶点（可堆叠）的概率随总量参数变化的临界行为。

Method: 在路径P_n上从总量为n·μ_n的所有弱组合中均匀取配置；利用树上精确的递归堆叠得分、一维路径消息递推、稀有二元亏损偏移的二分渐近、常数代价再生论证以及精确深消息必要性定理；并通过对独立几何占据条件于其和来还原均匀固定总量模型。

Result: 证明对数密度的定偏移双侧相变：对任意固定ε>0，当log₂μ_n最终≤中心−ε时堆叠概率趋于0，当最终≥中心+ε时趋于1；中心的零点处不作断言。有限确定性必要性定理及固定总量推论已在Lean中形式化并注册于Palomar，完整概率渐近定理未纳入该注册。

Conclusion: 路径上支撑坍塌图卵石模型在阈值附近呈现尖锐但对数尺度的相变，中心由√(log₂n)−½log₂log₂n+log₂(3e)给出，证明方法结合递归得分、消息递推与二分渐近，并部分实现形式化验证。

Abstract: We study a support-collapse version of graph pebbling on paths. A configuration is stackable if a sequence of legal pebbling moves can produce a nonzero configuration supported on a single vertex. On the path P_n, we choose a configuration uniformly from all weak compositions of total n times mu_n, where mu_n is a positive integer. We prove a two-sided fixed-offset transition for the logarithmic density. The transition is centred at sqrt(log_2 n) - (1/2) log_2 log_2 n + log_2(3e). For every fixed epsilon greater than zero, the stackability probability tends to zero when log_2 mu_n is eventually at most the centre minus epsilon, and tends to one when it is eventually at least the centre plus epsilon. No assertion is made at zero offset. The proof uses an exact recursive stackability score on trees, a one-dimensional path-message recurrence, binary-partition asymptotics for rare dyadic deficit excursions, a constant-cost regeneration argument, and an exact deep-message necessity theorem. Conditioning independent geometric occupancies on their sum returns the uniform fixed-total model. The finite deterministic necessity theorem and its exact fixed-total corollary are formalised in Lean and registered with Palomar; the full probabilistic asymptotic theorem is not part of that registration.

</details>


### [136] [Additive codes arising from hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39680)
*Gianira N. Alfarano*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文借助与加法码关联的整数多拟阵研究其临界指数，给出Whittle临界定理的编码论证明、h-投影系统刻画及一般界，并将超图多拟阵类加法码的临界指数、最小距离等参数与超图的弱色数、Berge围长、边连通性等联系起来，确定达到Singleton界的忠实超图加法准MDS码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究加法码的临界指数（critical exponent）这一核心参数，试图用整数多拟阵（integer polymatroid）作为统一工具刻画其性质，并把编码理论与拟阵/超图理论中的经典结果（如Whittle临界定理、Kung围长界）相互翻译与推广。

Method: 将加法码与整数多拟阵关联：给出编码论版本的Whittle临界定理证明；用h-投影系统（h-projective systems）几何刻画临界指数；建立一般界及Kung围长界的类比；针对多拟阵为超图H的超图多拟阵的加法码进行研究；将Griesmer界与线性规划界特殊化到超图码；利用加权2-截面（weighted 2-section）的Laplacian特征值推导最小距离下界，并进行计算比较。

Result: 对超图多拟阵加法码：临界指数由H的弱色数（weak chromatic number）决定；若码是忠实的（faithful），对偶码的最小折叠Hamming重量等于H的Berge围长；若H连通，则最小距离等于H的边连通性（edge-connectivity）。由此对h≥2确定所有H连通且达到Singleton界的此类码，即全部忠实超图加法准MDS码。此外还得到Griesmer界、线性规划界在超图码上的特化，以及基于Laplacian特征值的最小距离下界，并对各界的紧致性做了计算比较。

Conclusion: 整数多拟阵为研究加法码的临界指数提供了有力框架，可将编码参数与超图的弱色数、Berge围长、边连通性等组合量精确对应，统一并推广了若干经典界，并完整刻画了超图类忠实加法准MDS码。

Abstract: We study the critical exponent of additive codes through an integer polymatroid associated with the code. We give a coding-theoretic proof of Whittle's Critical Theorem in this setting, a geometric description of the critical exponent in terms of $h$-projective systems, and general bounds, including an analogue of Kung's girth bound. We then study additive codes whose polymatroid is the hypergraphic polymatroid of a hypergraph $H$. For these codes the critical exponent turns to be determined by the weak chromatic number of $H$. If the code is faithful, then the minimum folded Hamming weight of the dual code is equal to the Berge girth of $H$. If $H$ is connected, the minimum distance is equal to the edge-connectivity of $H$. As a consequence, for $h\geq2$ we determine all such codes with connected $H$ that attain the Singleton bound, that is, all faithful hypergraphic additive quasi-MDS codes. We specialise the Griesmer and linear programming bounds to hypergraphic codes, we derive a lower bound on the minimum distance from the Laplacian eigenvalues of the weighted $2$-section of $H$, and we compare all these bounds computationally.

</details>


### [137] [$(2,\mathcal{F})$-Avoiding Coloring and B-Coloring under Bipartite Exclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39716)
*Zhijun Lu,Qirui Ying,Huimin Song*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究F-free图的(2,F)-avoiding着色与B-着色，将两类着色问题统一归约为辅助超图中的P-完美匹配问题，借助Delcourt-Postle禁用于匹配定理，给出χ_{2,F}(G)与q_B(G)在充分大最大度Δ下的渐近上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chuet在其工作中研究了frugal着色并提出Problem A与Problem C。本文希望将相关结果从frugal着色推广到更一般的(2,F)-avoiding着色，并在B-着色（每条4-圈均为彩虹的边着色）情形下给出相应界，正面且尖锐地回答Chuet的问题。

Method: 设F为连通二部图，k(F)为删去某一部类中最小顶点集使F成为森林的规模。令m为F中成员的最少边数。核心方法是将(2,F)-avoiding着色和B-着色统一归约为辅助超图中的P-完美匹配问题，再运用Delcourt与Postle的forbidden-submatching定理进行分析。

Result: 若k(F)≤m-2，则最大度Δ充分大的F-free图满足χ_{2,F}(G)=O((Δ^m/logΔ)^{1/(m-1)})，正面且尖锐地回答Chuet的Problem A与C。对B-着色，令k=k(F)、h=|V(F)|、s=min{|X_F|,|Y_F|}，则q_B(G)不超过：s≤2时Δ+Δ^{1-η}+1；s≥3且k≤1时(4h-2)(Δ-1)+1；k≥2时C·Δ^{2-1/k}/logΔ，其中η,C仅依赖F。k≤1时线性阶最优，k≥2时该界接近尖锐。

Conclusion: 论文通过超图P-完美匹配的统一归约，将frugal着色结果推广到(2,F)-avoiding着色，并为F-free图的B-着色给出分情形上界；在线性阶情形下界最优，在k≥2时接近尖锐，从而对Chuet问题给出尖锐回答。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{F}$ be a nonempty family of connected bipartite graphs, each with at least two edges. For a graph $G$, a proper vertex coloring of $G$ is $(2,\mathcal{F})$-avoiding if no member of $\mathcal{F}$ occurs bichromatically, and $χ_{2,\mathcal{F}}(G)$ denotes the minimum number of colors in such a coloring. A B-coloring of $G$ is a proper edge-coloring in which every $4$-cycle is rainbow, and $q_B(G)$ denotes the minimum number of colors in a B-coloring of $G$. For a fixed connected bipartite graph $F$ with at least one edge and bipartition classes $X_F$ and $Y_F$, define $k(F)=\min\{|I|:I\subseteq X_F\text{ or }I\subseteq Y_F,F-I\text{ is a forest}\}$.
  Let $m\ge2$ be the minimum number of edges in a member of $\mathcal{F}$. We prove that if $k(F)\le m-2$, then every $F$-free graph $G$ of sufficiently large maximum degree $Δ$ satisfies $χ_{2,\mathcal{F}}(G)=O((\frac{Δ^m}{\logΔ})^{\frac{1}{m-1}})$, which gives a positive answer to Chuet's Problem A and C in a sharp sense, thereby extending the results of Chuet [arXiv:2603.23379] from frugal colorings to $(2,\mathcal{F})$-avoiding colorings.
  For B-colorings, put $k=k(F)$, $h=|V(F)|$, and $s=\min\{|X_F|,|Y_F|\}$. We prove that every $F$-free graph $G$ of sufficiently large maximum degree $Δ$ satisfies \[ q_B(G)\le \begin{cases} Δ+Δ^{1-η}+1, & \text{if }s\le2,\\ (4h-2)(Δ-1)+1, & \text{if }s\ge3\text{ and }k\le1,\\ C\frac{Δ^{2-\frac{1}{k}}}{\logΔ}, & \text{if }k\ge2, \end{cases} \] where $η>0$ and $C>0$ depend only on $F$. For $k\le1$, the linear order is best possible, and for $k\ge2$, the bound is nearly sharp.
  To prove these results, we develop a common reduction of the coloring problems to $P$-perfect matching problems in auxiliary hypergraphs and apply the forbidden-submatching theorem of Delcourt and Postle.

</details>


### [138] [Log-concavity and Approximate Counting for Totally Unimodular Polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39917)
*Jonathan Leake,Maryam Mohammadi Yekta*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出了所有全单模多胞体格点数量的新下界，推广了列联表与整数流等已有结果，并据此得到高效确定性近似计数算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有下界多针对列联表、整数流等特定对象，缺乏对一般全单模多胞体格点数的统一刻画与高效近似计数方法，同时Barvinok对数凹性猜想及Ferroni–Higashitani关于Ehrhart多项式求值的猜想尚未解决。

Method: 基于Gurvits容量凸优化问题构造下界；证明相关生成多项式属于一类新的对数凹多项式——变量逐项对数凹（VLC）；核心是解决直线上列联表的Barvinok对数凹性猜想，并进一步提出其推广猜想。

Result: 得到全单模多胞体格点数的新下界，并给出至显式指数因子的高效确定性近似计数算法；验证了Ferroni与Higashitani关于单模多胞体Ehrhart多项式求值的猜想。

Conclusion: 通过VLC多项式框架与Gurvits容量方法，统一并强化了全单模多胞体格点计数的下界与近似算法，并提出了可望带来更强、更一般界的新猜想。

Abstract: We present a new lower bound on the number of lattice points of all totally unimodular polytopes, generalizing previous lower bounds on contingency tables, integer flows, and beyond. Our bound is based on the Gurvits capacity convex optimization problem, and thus our result implies an efficient deterministic algorithm for approximate counting of the lattice points up to an explicit exponential factor. We achieve our bounds by showing that the associated generating polynomials fit into a new general class of log-concave polynomials called VLC ("variable-wise log-concavity''). This also implies a conjecture of Ferroni and Higashitani on the evaluations of the Ehrhart polynomials of unimodular polytopes. The essential ingredient for these results is the resolution of Barvinok's log-concavity conjecture for contingency tables on lines, which was proven using ChatGPT 6 Astra. We conjecture a generalization of Barvinok's conjecture, which we believe will lead to stronger and more general bounds.

</details>


### [139] [Involutions avoiding 4321 and another pattern of length four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39952)
*Marilena Barnabei,Niccolò Castronuovo,Matteo Silimbani*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文枚举了同时避开一个长度为4的经典模式与4321的所有对合族，给出双变量生成函数与结构刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 避开两个经典模式的置换枚举已较为成熟，但对合情形有额外的结构约束，需要专门技术。作者借此填补该空白。

Method: 主要工具是Biane在对合与带标号Motzkin路径之间的双射，结合大量实验与软件辅助方法进行枚举。

Result: 给出精确双变量生成函数（计入长度与下降数）及除一例之外所有族的结构描述；剩余情形给出泛函方程并表示为显式连分数。

Conclusion: 该工作通过对合与带标号Motzkin路径的双射，系统解决了这类模式避免对合的枚举问题。

Abstract: We enumerate all families of involutions avoiding a classical pattern of length four together with 4321. While the enumeration of permutations avoiding two classical patterns is by now well understood, the case of pattern-avoiding involutions presents additional structural constraints that require dedicated techniques. In particular our main enumerative tool is Biane's bijection between involutions and labelled Motzkin paths.
  The paper provides exact bivariate generating functions (taking into account length and number of descents), and structural descriptions for all except one family. In the remaining case we provide a functional equation for the generating function and express it as an explicit continued fraction.
  We make extensive use of experimental and software-supported methods.

</details>


### [140] [Separating Notions of Graph Width: the Adaptive, Normal, Linear, Entropic, and Submodular Width](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40018)
*Matthias Lanzinger,Timo Camillo Merkl,Dan Suciu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 构造了一个32个顶点的显式简单图G，使其自适应宽度、正规宽度、线性宽度、熵宽度与子模宽度两两不同；除熵宽度仅给出上下界外，其余宽度均精确计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图的各类宽度（自适应、正规、线性、熵、子模）之间的分离关系，此前缺乏一个能够将这五种宽度两两区分的显式实例。

Method: 给出一个32个顶点的显式简单图；利用Ingleton不等式与Zhang-Yeung不等式推导上界；并通过显式构造模、正规、线性、熵与非熵多拟阵（polymatroid）来获得下界。

Result: 计算结果：除熵宽度仅给出下界与上界外，其余四种宽度均被精确求出，且五种宽度在该图上两两不同。

Conclusion: 该32顶点图提供了一个将自适应、正规、线性、熵与子模宽度完全区分开的显式例子，验证了这些宽度层级间的严格分离。

Abstract: We describe one explicit simple graph G on 32 vertices whose adaptive, normal, linear, entropic, and submodular widths are pairwise distinct. We compute all these widths exactly, except for the entropic width, where we only give a lower and upper bound. We use Ingleton's inequality and the Zhang-Yeung inequality for upper bounds, and give explicit constructions of modular, normal, linear, entropic, and non-entropic polymatroids for lower bounds.

</details>


### [141] [On equidistributed directions in finite affine planes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40023)
*Sam Adriaensen,Bence Csajbók,Zsuzsa Weiner*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究仿射平面中"确定方向"的推广，给出在任何有限平移平面中恰好从3个方向非等分布的（多重）点集构造并证明其为全部解；同时用代数图论方法研究点集对的方向性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定方向（determined directions）是仿射平面中的经典概念，Kiss 与 Somlai 在素数阶 Desarguesian 平面中已有结果。本文旨在将相关研究推广到任意有限平移平面，并借助代数图论工具处理点集对的方向关联问题。

Method: 1) 采用线在无穷远上的点（平行类）刻画方向，定义点（多重）集从某方向的等分布/非等分布；2) 构造恰好从3个方向非等分布的点（多重）集，并证明其完备性；3) 运用代数图论（结合线性代数与图论）研究点集对 S、T 的方向性质；4) 附录给出证明 |S∩T|=|S||T|/q² 的组合替代方法；5) 引入集合对"交叉确定方向"的概念并建立不等式。

Result: 1) 给出任意有限平移平面中恰好从3个方向非等分布的点（多重）集的构造，并证明所有此类集合均由此构造产生，推广了 Kiss–Somlai 在素数阶 Desarguesian 平面的结果；2) 若对每个方向，S 或 T 至少有一个关于该方向等分布，则 |S∩T|=|S||T|/q²；3) 若 (S,T) 交叉确定至多一半方向，则 |S||T|≤q²，且等号成立当且仅当 S=T。

Conclusion: 本文将确定方向的研究从素数阶 Desarguesian 平面推广至任意有限平移平面，完整刻画了恰好3个非等分布方向的多重集结构；并通过代数图论与组合方法，建立了点集对方向性质与交集大小、集合积之间的定量关系。

Abstract: We investigate a recently introduced generalisation of determined directions in affine planes. A direction $(d)$ in an affine plane is a point of the line at infinity in the projective completion, and hence corresponds to a parallel class. A (multi)set $S$ of points is equidistributed from direction $(d)$ if all lines from that parallel class intersect $S$ in the same number of points. In this paper, we give a construction of point (multi)sets that are inequidistributed from exactly 3 directions in any finite translation plane, and prove that all such (multi)sets arise from this construction, generalising a result of Kiss and Somlai in Desarguesian planes of prime order. We also use ideas from algebraic graph theory to prove results on directions associated with a pair of point sets $S$ and $T$. If for every direction $(d)$ either $S$ or $T$ is equidistributed from $(d)$, we prove that $|S \cap T| = |S||T|/q^2$, where $q$ is the order of the affine plane. In the appendix, we give a combinatorial alternative approach to proving this equality, which can be of independent interest. We also introduce the notion of directions cross-determined by a pair of sets $S$ and $T$, and prove that $|S| |T| \leq q^2$ if $(S,T)$ cross-determines at most half of the directions, where equality forces $S = T$.

</details>


### [142] [Unimodality of Kazhdan--Lusztig polynomials of sparse paving matroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40084)
*Matthew H. Y. Xie,Zuo-Ru Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了无环稀疏铺砌拟阵的Kazhdan-Lusztig多项式是单峰的，并在秩至少为5且余秩为正时给出严格单峰性及三个退化例外情形的完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kazhdan-Lusztig多项式的系数单峰性是组合学与代数组合中的核心问题。拟阵（尤其是稀疏铺砌拟阵）是该理论的重要研究对象，但对其Kazhdan-Lusztig多项式单峰性的认识尚不完整，本文旨在填补无环稀疏铺砌拟阵这一情形的空白。

Method: 对无环稀疏铺砌拟阵的Kazhdan-Lusztig多项式进行系数分析；通过刻画其系数序列结构，讨论单峰性与严格单峰性，并在秩≥5、余秩>0的条件下确定所有破坏严格单峰性的参数三元组，同时分析模式位置并分类退化情形。

Result: 证明了无环稀疏铺砌拟阵的Kazhdan-Lusztig多项式是单峰的；当秩≥5且余秩为正时，系数序列严格单峰，仅有三个明确确定的参数三元组为例外，每个例外给出两个相等的最大系数；进一步确定了众数位置并对正秩、正余秩的无环稀疏铺砌退化拟阵进行了分类。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了无环稀疏铺砌拟阵Kazhdan-Lusztig多项式的单峰行为，明确了严格单峰性的唯一例外情形并给出退化拟阵的分类，推广了相关单峰性结果。

Abstract: We prove that the Kazhdan--Lusztig polynomial of a loopless sparse paving matroid is unimodal. When the rank is at least five and the corank is positive, we show that the coefficient sequence is strictly unimodal except for three explicitly determined parameter triples, each giving two equal maximal coefficients. We also determine the modes and classify the degenerate loopless sparse paving matroids of positive rank and corank.

</details>


### [143] [On the solution to the Erdős-Hajnal problem on high-girth high-chromatic subgraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40192)
*Tung Nguyen,Bartosz Walczak*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文阐释Kohlmeyer-Kruer构造方法并给出优化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应Erdős-Hajnal关于大色数图中是否含大围长且大色数子图的问题。

Method: 系统阐释Kohlmeyer-Kruer的构造方法，并将其与相关文献联系起来。

Result: 在无四环子图色数上界方面，将原构造中的6优化至3。

Conclusion: 给出Erdős-Hajnal问题的强否定解法，并改进了相关界的常数。

Abstract: A well-known problem of Erdős and Hajnal from the 1960s asks whether every graph with huge chromatic number contains a subgraph with large girth and large chromatic number. Very recently, Kohlmeyer and Kruer provided a strong negative solution to this problem: a construction of triangle-free graphs with arbitrarily large chromatic number whose subgraphs with no four-cycle have chromatic number at most $6$. The purpose of this exposition is to explain the construction method, relate it to relevant literature, and optimise the bound `$6$' to `$3$'.

</details>


### [144] [Positive cubature on $S^2$:low-degree rigidity and uniform bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40210)
*Zhuo Cheng,Deyu Yu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文研究球面 \(S^2\) 上正求积公式的最少节点数 \(N_t\)，证明奇数度数 \(2m+1\) 的公式不能有恰好 \((m+1)(m+2)+1\) 个节点，从而排除 Fisher 界取等情形，得到 \(N_{2m+1}\ge (m+1)(m+2)+2\)（\(m\ge3\)），并给出 \(N_7=22,N_9=32\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定球面正求积公式的最小节点数 \(N_t\) 是球面积分逼近中的核心难题；Fisher 界给出下界，但取等问题长期未完全澄清，尤其奇数度数情形需要更精细的排除与改进。

Method: 构造方面，利用 Lobatto 纬度上的正圆测度给出奇数度数的上界，含二次系数 \(13/8\)、奇偶相关线性项与 \(O(m^{2/3})\) 余项，并确定标量余项的精确常数 \(49\sqrt[3]{3}/72\)；下界方面，使用连续加权 LP–Turán 不等式，以及其 Helmholtz 伴随为非负测度的径向帽，证明帽泛函在固定容许支撑半径处存在极大元，并通过保正性转移到球面得到显式有限度数下界。

Result: 证明度数 \(2m+1\) 的公式对任意 \(m\ge2\) 均不可能恰有 \((m+1)(m+2)+1\) 个节点；由排除 Fisher 界等号得 \(N_{2m+1}\ge (m+1)(m+2)+2\)（\(m\ge3\)），且已知构造给出精确值 \(N_7=22\)、\(N_9=32\)；一般奇数度数上界具有二次系数 \(13/8\)、奇偶相关线性项与 \(O(m^{2/3})\) 余项，标量余项精确常数为 \(49\sqrt[3]{3}/72\)。

Conclusion: 该工作排除了奇数度数下 Fisher 界的等号可能，改进了球面正求积公式最小节点数的下界，并精确刻画了 Lobatto 纬度构造的渐近余项常数；同时建立了基于 LP–Turán 不等式与径向帽的下界框架，为确定一般 \(N_t\) 提供新工具。

Abstract: Let \(N_t\) denote the least number of nodes in a positive cubature formula of degree \(t\) on \(S^2\). We prove that a formula of degree \(2m+1\) cannot have exactly \((m+1)(m+2)+1\) nodes for any \(m\ge2\). Excluding equality in the Fisher bound then gives \[ N_{2m+1}\ge (m+1)(m+2)+2 \] for \(m\ge3\), and known constructions yield the exact values \(N_7=22\) and \(N_9=32\). For general odd degrees, positive circle measures on Lobatto latitudes give an upper bound with quadratic coefficient \(13/8\), parity-dependent linear terms, and an \(O(m^{2/3})\) remainder. We determine the sharp constant \(49\sqrt[3]{3}/72\) for the scalar remainder in this construction. Lower bounds are obtained from continuous weighted LP--Turán inequalities and radial caps whose Helmholtz companions are nonnegative measures. We prove that the cap functional admits a maximizer at each fixed admissible support radius and derive explicit finite-degree lower bounds by a positivity-preserving transfer to the sphere.

</details>


### [145] [Classification of prime graphs with 2-switch-degree at most 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40274)
*Victor N. Schvöllner*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究图的2-交换度（即实现图中顶点的度），对2-交换度不超过4的素图进行分类，并由此导出实现图全局形状可由单个局部度决定的二分定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现图 \mathcal{G}(d) 的全局结构刻画困难，而2-交换度作为其顶点的局部度量，可能决定整体结构。作者希望通过对低2-交换度素图的分类，建立局部度与全局图结构之间的联系。

Method: 定义2-交换度为图上可行2-交换的数量，等价于度序列d的实现图 \mathcal{G}(d) 中对应顶点的度；引入素图概念（对Tyshkevich分解不可分解且每个顶点参与某个2-交换），对2-交换度至多4的素图进行分类研究。

Result: 得到2-交换度至多4的素图的完整分类；由此证明锐二分：若d有实现X满足 \text{deg}(X)=k\le3，则 \mathcal{G}(d) 顶点传递且k-正则当且仅当 X 不以 T_{221} 或其补 \overline{T_{221}} 为诱导子图；对每个 k\geq4，存在度为k的素图，其2-交换可使度升至2k-2；且素图实现图在同构意义下度至多4的仅10个。

Conclusion: 低2-交换度素图的分类揭示了实现图局部度与全局正则/顶点传递性质之间的锐二分关系，并给出了度至多4的素图实现图的完整有限列表。

Abstract: The 2-switch-degree $\text{deg}(G)$ of a graph $G$ is the number of 2-switches that can be performed on $G$; equivalently, it is the degree of $G$ as a vertex of the realization graph $\mathcal{G}(d)$ of its degree sequence $d$. We classify the prime graphs of 2-switch-degree at most 4, where a graph is prime if it is indecomposable with respect to the Tyshkevich composition and every vertex takes part in some 2-switch. From this classification we derive a sharp dichotomy that recovers the global shape of $\mathcal{G}(d)$ from a single one of its local degrees: if $d$ has a realization $X$ with $\text{deg}(X)=k\le3$, then $\mathcal{G}(d)$ is vertex-transitive and $k$-regular if and only if neither $T_{221}$ nor $\overline{T_{221}}$ is an induced subgraph of $X$. Moreover, for every $k\geq 4$ some prime graph of degree $k$ carries a 2-switch raising its degree to $2k-2$. As a further consequence, up to isomorphism there are only 10 realization graphs of prime graphs with degree at most 4.

</details>


### [146] [Another proof that the two color bipartite Ramsey number is $O(2^t)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40288)
*Yury Person*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文独立证明了二色二分拉姆齐数上界 b_2(t)≤128·2^t，并推广到一般 q：b_q(t)≤128·q^t，同时给出 Zarankiewicz 数的统一推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 K_{n,n} 边染色下出现单色 K_{t,t} 的临界规模 b_q(t)，即二分图的二色/多色拉姆齐数，并为 Zarankiewicz 数提供统一刻画。

Method: 给出独立证明：先证明更一般的密度版本——对 0<p≤1/2，任意边密度至少 p、且两侧顶点数至少 128·p^{-t} 的二部图必含 K_{t,t}；再将其应用于染色情形推出 b_2(t) 与 b_q(t) 的上界。

Result: b_2(t)≤128·2^t；密度命题成立；由此 b_q(t)≤128·q^t（q≥2），并得到关于 Zarankiewicz 数的统一推论。

Conclusion: 通过密度型二部图嵌入结果，统一给出二分拉姆齐数 b_q(t) 的线性于 q^t 的上界及 Zarankiewicz 数相关推论。

Abstract: For positive integers $t$ and $q$ let $b_q(t)$ be the smallest integer $n$ so that any coloring of the edges of the complete bipartite graph $K_{n,n}$ with $q$ colors yields a monochromatic copy of $K_{t,t}$. We give an independent proof that $b_2(t)\le 128\cdot 2^t$ for every positive integer $t$. More generally, for $0< p\le 1/2$, every bipartite graph of edge density at least $p$, with both classes of size at least $128\cdot p^{-t}$, contains $K_{t,t}$. This gives $b_q(t)\le 128\cdot q^t$ for every integer $q\ge 2$ and a uniform consequence for Zarankiewicz numbers.

</details>
